Across the experiments, CSV-ViT achieved higher classification performance than recent surface-based models. The results suggest that the proposed CSV-ViT may support MRI-based prediction of AD-related status prior to PET or CSF confirmation.

</details>


### [36] [InterSketch: An Interleaved Reasoning Model with Self-correcting Visual Sketch and Stepwise Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26520)
*Zhiwei Ning,Wenwen Tong,Xiangli Kong,Shengnan Ma,Ziyi Shang,Jingcheng Ni,Tao Hu,Yong Xien Chng,Jixuan Ying,Zehuan Wu,Hanming Deng,Jie Yang,Yuanjie Zheng,Wei Liu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterSketch：一种通过自校正和分步奖励机制增强视觉-文本交错思维链能力的交错推理模型，在视觉推理基准上表现出色


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型的多轮视觉推理轨迹相对较浅且以文本为中心，限制了其在复杂视觉挑战中的应用。而人类思维通常涉及具有交错视觉-文本思维链的长视界推理，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 1. 冷启动阶段：提出合成高质量的交错VT-CoT数据集，并包含反思机制以支持多轮交错推理和自校正；2. 强化学习阶段：设计分步奖励机制以缓解长视界推理中仅末端监督带来的奖励稀疏问题；3. 使用外部工具动态生成中间视觉草图，并与文本推理交错进行。

Result: 在视觉推理基准上的大量实验证明了InterSketch的有效性，甚至优于Gemini-3-Pro等专有模型。

Conclusion: InterSketch通过交错视觉-文本推理、自校正机制和分步奖励设计，成功增强了模型的长视界视觉理解能力，为复杂视觉挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) have exhibited multi-turn visual reasoning capabilities, their reasoning trajectories remain relatively shallow and are dominated by a text-centric paradigm, limiting their applicability to complex visual challenges. In contrast, human-like thought typically involves long-horizon reasoning with an interleaved visual-textual chain-of-thought (VT-CoT). To bridge this gap, we introduce InterSketch, an interleaved reasoning model to enhance the VT-CoT capability via self-correcting and stepwise reward mechanisms. InterSketch dynamically generates intermediate visual sketches using external tools and interleaves them with textual reasoning, enabling effective perception and logical reasoning over long-horizon visual understanding tasks. Specifically, in the first cold-start stage, we propose a synthesized high-quality interleaved VT-CoT dataset and include a reflection mechanism to enable the model's capability in multi-turn interleaved reasoning and self-correction. In the subsequent reinforcement learning (RL) stage, we design a stepwise reward mechanism to mitigate the sparsity of reward signals inherent in end-only supervision over long-horizon reasoning. Extensive experiments on visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of InterSketch, even outperforming proprietary models such as Gemini-3-Pro.

</details>


### [37] [CmIVTP: Cross-modal Interaction-based Vessel Trajectory Prediction for Maritime Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26524)
*Yuxu Lu,Dong Yang,Xiaoyu Li,Mengwei Bao,Congcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出名为CmIVTP的跨模态交互船舶轨迹预测框架，通过整合AIS和CCTV多模态数据解决单一数据源局限性，利用目标感知场景编码器和跨模态交互Transformer提升预测精度，并构建海事多模态数据集Maritime-MmD⁺


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事智能交通系统中船舶轨迹预测面临挑战，主要原因是单一数据源的限制：AIS数据对小船稀疏或不可用，CCTV数据无法完全捕捉船舶动态行为。需要多模态融合方法来解决这些局限性。

Method: 1) 提出目标感知场景编码器提取场景语义特征，捕捉船舶-环境交互；2) 设计跨模态交互Transformer，集成AIS运动特征、CCTV环境特征和场景表示，使用跨模态注意力机制；3) 构建船舶群轨迹库，聚类历史AIS轨迹为代表性运动模式；4) 创建海事多模态数据集Maritime-MmD⁺，同步AIS和CCTV数据。

Result: 广泛实验表明，CmIVTP在多模态驱动的船舶轨迹预测基准上取得了更好的性能。框架能够生成动态一致且环境可行的预测，通过整合多模态数据显著提高了预测准确性。

Conclusion: CmIVTP框架通过有效的跨模态交互建模，解决了海事轨迹预测中单一数据源的局限性。该方法结合了多模态数据融合、场景语义理解和历史模式挖掘，为海事智能交通系统的安全高效运行提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Maritime intelligent transportation systems (MITS) are essential for ensuring navigation safety and efficiency in busy waterways. However, accurate vessel trajectory prediction remains challenging due to the limitations of single-source data. Automatic identification system (AIS) data is often sparse or unavailable for small vessels, while closed-circuit television (CCTV) data alone cannot fully capture dynamic vessel behavior. To mitigate these challenges, we propose a cross-modal interaction-based vessel trajectory prediction (named CmIVTP) framework to model the intricate interactions between vessel dynamics and environmental constraints. Specifically, we introduce a target-aware scene encoder to extract scene semantic features, effectively capturing vessel-environment interactions and enhancing trajectory prediction accuracy. In addition, we propose a cross-modal interaction transformer, which integrates AIS-derived motion features, CCTV-based environmental features, and scene representations. It leverages cross-modal attention mechanisms to simultaneously capture intra-modal semantics and inter-modal interactions, ensuring dynamically consistent and environmentally feasible predictions. Furthermore, we construct a vessel group trajectory bank by clustering historical AIS trajectories into representative motion patterns, providing an efficient and scalable approach for candidate trajectory generation. Additionally, we introduce the maritime multimodal dataset plus (named Maritime-MmD$^+$), a large-scale dataset that synchronizes AIS data and CCTV video data, providing robust support for multimodal trajectory prediction research. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CmIVTP achieves better performance on multimodal-driven vessel trajectory prediction benchmarks. The code resources for this work can be available at https://github.com/LouisYxLu/CmIVTP.

</details>


### [38] [ReCA: Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation via Recursive Context Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26525)
*Akide Liu,Jinbo Xing,Chaojie Mao,Ye Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Weijie Wang,Zihan Wang,Yu Liu,Gholamreza Haffari,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了多镜头视频外推任务和递归上下文分配框架，解决分钟级电影视频生成中的长期一致性挑战，通过分层上下文分配显著提升了多镜头一致性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成范式只能解决分钟级电影视频生成挑战的部分问题：单镜头外推能保持锚点但缺乏电影结构，而多镜头故事讲述有结构但会自由创造视觉状态而非延续观察到的状态。需要一种既能保持锚点状态又能推进叙事意图的多镜头视频生成方法。

Method: 提出了递归上下文分配框架：1) 将多镜头视频外推任务递归分解为上下文有界的子问题；2) 在叶节点调用冻结生成器；3) 在时间维度传播结构化状态更新。该方法在推理时分层分配上下文到规划和生成过程。

Result: 相比先前方法，ReCA将平均归一化分数提升了8%到16%，多镜头一致性指标提升了28%到43%。该方法揭示了长视频失败不仅是上下文长度限制，更是上下文分配失败的问题。

Conclusion: 通过定义多镜头视频外推任务并提出递归上下文分配框架，成功解决了分钟级电影视频生成的核心挑战。该框架通过分层上下文分配有效解决了全局规划过度指定、镜头级提示稀释和时态链接导致状态衰减等瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Minute-scale cinematic video generation is a central challenge for generative video models. Existing paradigms address only fragments of this challenge: single-shot extrapolation preserves an anchor but lacks cinematic structure, while multi-shot storytelling imposes structure yet remains free to invent its visual states rather than continue an observed one. We define Multi-Shot Video Extrapolation (MSVE), a task that extends an observed frame or clip into a sequence of cinematically structured shots while preserving anchor state and advancing narrative intent. This setting operates under the finite per-call generation budget of short-video models. We identify three coupled bottlenecks: (1) global planners over-specify unsupported details from full screenplays; (2) shot-level prompts dilute task-relevant state when carrying the complete story; and (3) temporal chaining turns generated frames into a lossy memory in which identity, scene, object, and action state decay. MSVE reveals that long-video failure is not merely a limitation of context length, but a failure of context allocation. We propose Recursive Context Allocation (ReCA), an inference-time framework that allocates context hierarchically across planning and generation. ReCA recursively decomposes MSVE into context-bounded subproblems, invokes frozen generators at leaf nodes, and propagates structured state updates across time. To evaluate this setting, we further propose MSVE-Bench and NB-Q, a source-grounded protocol with prompts purpose-built for 3 to 5 minute long-video generation, a regime not addressed by existing short-clip benchmarks. Compared to previous methods, ReCA improves average normalized score by 8 to 16 percent over the strongest competing controller and improves multi-shot consistency metrics by 28 to 43 percent. View the project page at https://reca.vmv.re.

</details>


### [39] [TrackRef3D: Multi-View Consistent Track-then-Label for Open-World Referring Segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26576)
*Yuyang Tan,Renhe Zhang,Hang Zhang,Ao Li,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackRef3D：一个完全自动化的3D高斯泼溅中开放世界参考分割方法，无需手动标注，通过轨迹感知语义共识和混合训练策略实现多视图一致性和查询鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅中的参考分割方法依赖于昂贵的逐场景手动标注和逐视图伪掩码生成，存在多视图不一致性和对查询特异性泛化能力差的问题。需要一种无需手动标注、能确保多视图一致性并具有良好泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出了TrackRef3D，一个完全自动化的流水线，采用"先跟踪后标注"的多视图一致范式，将对象发现与语义基础解耦。具体包括：轨迹感知语义共识模块（TSCM）通过同义词聚类和轨迹感知投票建立规范语义身份；可见性感知描述生成策略减少歧义；混合训练策略（HTS）通过多正样本对比目标联合优化粗粒度类别语义和细粒度参考线索。

Result: 在基准测试中的大量实验表明，TrackRef3D达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: TrackRef3D通过创新的轨迹感知语义共识和混合训练策略，成功实现了无需手动标注的3D高斯泼溅开放世界参考分割，解决了多视图不一致性和查询特异性泛化问题，为具身AI提供了重要能力。

Abstract: Referring 3D Gaussian Splatting (R3DGS), which utilizes natural language for 3D object segmentation, has emerged as a crucial capability for embodied AI. However, existing methods typically rely on expensive per-scene manual annotation and per-view pseudo mask generation, which suffer from multi-view inconsistency and poor generalization to varying query specificities. To address this, we present TrackRef3D, a fully automatic pipeline that achieves open-world referring segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) without manual annotation by introducing a multi-view consistent track-then-label paradigm that fundamentally decouples object discovery from semantic grounding. Specifically, we propose a Trajectory-Aware Semantic Consensus Module (TSCM) which aggregates cross-view predictions via synonymous clustering and trajectory-aware voting to establish a canonical semantic identity, thereby ensuring multi-view consistency. Furthermore, we employ a visibility-aware description generation strategy to mitigate ambiguity and propose a Hybrid Training Strategy (HTS) that jointly optimizes coarse category semantics and fine-grained referential cues to ensure robustness under varying query specificities using a multi-positive contrastive objective. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that TrackRef3D achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [40] [O-MARC: Omni Memory-Augmented Compression Distillation for Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26584)
*Peiran Wu,Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Chen Chen,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UGC-AVQA基准测试用于评估音频视频关联理解，并开发OMAC无训练压缩方法和O-MARC压缩蒸馏框架，在降低推理成本的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全模态大语言模型虽然能实现统一的音频视频理解，但存在两个主要问题：1) 长联合标记序列导致推理成本高昂；2) 现有基准测试无法在嘈杂的用户生成视频中完全隔离音频视觉关联。

Method: 提出三种方法：1) UGC-AVQA基准测试，包含1,000个视频和4,816个问答对，通过音频移除测试确保问题需要音视频证据；2) OMAC无需训练的可插拔压缩方法，保留显著的视觉记忆和时间定位的音频锚点；3) O-MARC压缩蒸馏框架，用于学习记忆压缩的多模态上下文。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B上，O-MARC将四个基准测试的平均分数提升至45.8，优于完整标记推理的44.1和OmniZip的41.0。OMAC将延迟降低34.6%（1.53倍加速），内存使用减少34.7%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一套系统方法解决全模态模型的高计算成本和评估难题，通过创新的压缩技术和专门的基准测试，实现了在保持性能的同时显著降低推理成本，为高效的多模态理解提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Omnimodal large language models enable unified audio video understanding, but long joint token sequences make inference costly, and existing benchmarks do not fully isolate audio visual association in noisy user generated videos. We introduce UGC-AVQA, a public UGC benchmark with 1,000 videos and 4,816 QA pairs, where an audio removal test ensures that benchmark questions require both acoustic and visual evidence. To reduce inference cost, we propose OMAC, a training free plug in compression method that preserves salient visual memory and temporally grounded audio anchors. To further make compact models robust to compressed inputs, we introduce O-MARC, a compression distillation framework for learning with memory compressed multimodal contexts. On Qwen2.5-Omni-3B, O-MARC improves the average score across four benchmarks to 45.8, outperforming full token inference at 44.1 and OmniZip at 41.0. OMAC also keeps inference efficient, reducing latency by 34.6\% (1.53$\times$ speedup) and memory by 34.7\% compared with full token inference.

</details>


### [41] [FTibSuite: A Comprehensive Resource Suite for Tibetan Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26601)
*Guixian Xu,Yide Liang,Zeli Su,Xuexian Song,Ziyin Zhang,Yushuang Dong,Ting Zhang,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FTibSuite是一个针对藏语的综合性视觉-语言研究资源套件，包括训练数据、评估基准和基线模型，填补了藏语作为低资源语言在视觉-语言模型领域的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藏语作为一种低资源语言，在视觉-语言模型领域严重缺乏可复现的训练和评估基础设施。虽然视觉-语言模型发展迅速，但藏语相关研究仍存在重大缺口，需要系统性的资源建设。

Method: 提出了FTibSuite三部分框架：1) FTibData - 包含持续预训练、图像-文本对齐和指令调优数据的人类验证多模态训练语料库；2) FTibBench - 基于五个主流多模态基准的藏语适配版本，采用分层质量控制工作流减少翻译噪声；3) FTibVLM - 基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct通过三阶段适配管道构建的可复现基线模型。

Result: 在FTibBench上的实验显示，FTibVLM在所有任务上都取得了持续的性能提升：MMBench准确率从42.97提升到67.78，POPE-random准确率从47.53提升到80.56。同时保持了骨干模型原有的中文能力，性能退化最小。这为藏语多模态研究提供了首个标准化基础。

Conclusion: FTibSuite成功填补了藏语视觉-语言研究的资源空白，提供了一套完整的训练数据、评估基准和基线模型。该方法有效提升了藏语多模态任务的性能，同时保持了原有语言能力，为未来的藏语多模态研究奠定了标准化基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models have progressed rapidly, but Tibetan remains a severely underserved low-resource language due to the lack of reproducible training and evaluation infrastructure. To fill this gap, we introduce FTibSuite, a comprehensive resource suite for Tibetan vision-language research, consisting of FTibData (human-verified multimodal training corpora spanning continual pretraining, image-text alignment, and instruction tuning data), FTibBench (Tibetan adaptations of five mainstream multimodal benchmarks with a hierarchical quality-control workflow to reduce translation noise), and FTibVLM, a reproducible baseline built on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct via a three-stage adaptation pipeline. Experiments on FTibBench show FTibVLM delivers consistent performance gains across all tasks, such as improving MMBench accuracy from 42.97 to 67.78 and POPE-random accuracy from 47.53 to 80.56, while retaining the backbone's original Chinese capabilities with minimal degradation, providing the first standardized foundation for Tibetan multimodal research.

</details>


### [42] [Gaussian-Voxel Duet: A Dual-Scaffolding Hybrid Representation for Fast and Accurate Monocular Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26616)
*Zhenhua Du,Zhen Tan,Haoyu Zhang,Dewen Hu,Shuaifeng Zhi,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VoxelGS方法，通过将高斯锚定到稀疏体素支架上，解决了3D高斯喷洒中几何精度与优化效率的权衡问题，实现了高质量表面重建和实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯喷洒技术在逼真新视角合成方面取得了显著成功，但长期以来一直受到几何精度与优化效率之间权衡的制约。专注于图像渲染的方法虽然收敛快，但由于多余基元对训练视图的过拟合导致几何不完美；而整合神经有符号距离场（SDF）以获得更好几何的方法则训练成本过高。

Method: 提出了一种混合高斯-体素表示方法，将锚定高斯与联合优化的稀疏体素支架绑定。这种方法将锚定高斯明确限制在由体素化SDF定义的表面附近窄带内，有效提高了表示效率并在不牺牲几何质量的情况下凝聚了漂浮高斯。还引入了隐式表面绑定损失，以相互正则化的方式将单个高斯基元拉近到SDF诱导的表面，提高重建精度。

Result: 在ScanNet++、ScanNetv2和DeepBlending数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在表面重建质量方面达到了最先进水平，在新视角合成方面优于领先基线方法，同时保持了快速训练收敛和实时渲染能力。

Conclusion: VoxelGS方法通过创新的混合表示和正则化策略，成功解决了3D高斯喷洒中几何精度与优化效率的权衡问题，实现了高质量3D重建的同时保持了效率优势，为3D场景表示提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic novel view synthesis, its pursuit of fast and high-fidelity 3D reconstruction has long been constrained by a trade-off between geometric accuracy and optimization efficiency. Methods specialized in image rendering converge quickly at the cost of imperfect geometry caused by superfluous primitives overfitting training views, while methods integrating neural signed-distance field (SDF) for better geometry incur prohibitive training costs. In this paper, we attempt to strike a better trade-off by tethering scaffold-anchored Gaussians to a jointly optimized sparse voxel scaffold. This hybrid Gaussian-Voxel representation explicitly confines anchored Gaussians to a narrow band around surfaces defined by voxelized SDFs, which effectively improves representation efficiency and condenses floating Gaussians without sacrificing geometry quality. An implicit surface tethering loss further pulls individual Gaussian primitives closer to SDF-induced surfaces in a mutually regularized manner for improved reconstruction accuracy. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world indoor scenes from ScanNet++, ScanNetv2, and DeepBlending datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art surface reconstruction quality as well as superior novel view synthesis against leading baselines, while maintaining fast training convergence and real-time rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/duzh11/VoxelGS.

</details>


### [43] [MedVol-R1: Reward-Driven Evidence Grounding for Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26621)
*Zichun Wang,Hairong Shi,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu,Zihua Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedVol-R1是一个基于强化学习的3D医学图像分割框架，通过将临床推理与2D证据锚点（关键轴向切片和2D边界框）显式分离，然后通过冻结的MedSAM2模块传播到3D掩码，实现体积推理分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的体积推理分割方法通常依赖专门的分割令牌来连接语言和掩码解码，但这种耦合将决策过程压缩为不透明的潜在表示，限制了可解释性和对不同叙述表达式的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了MedVol-R1框架：1) 使用LVLM（大视觉语言模型）将临床推理接地到可验证的2D证据锚点（关键轴向切片和2D边界框）；2) 通过冻结的MedSAM2模块将2D证据传播到连贯的3D掩码；3) 采用冷启动监督微调后接GRPO强化学习训练，使用多组件奖励函数鼓励信息丰富的证据选择、准确的2D空间接地和跨切片体积一致性。

Result: 在M3D-Seg基准测试中的CT-ORG、AbdomenCT-1K和KiTS23数据集上，MedVol-R1始终优于强基线方法并达到最先进性能，强化学习相比纯监督微调提供了明确的性能提升。

Conclusion: MedVol-R1通过显式解耦证据接地和体积描绘，提供了一种可解释且有效的体积推理分割方法，避免了昂贵的思维链标注需求，并在多个医学图像分割基准上展示了优越性能。

Abstract: Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to segment a target region in a 3D medical scan from a free-form clinical query, where the referent is often implicit and requires both medical knowledge and volume-grounded reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on specialized segmentation tokens to connect language with mask decoding, but this coupling collapses the decision process into opaque latent representations, limiting interpretability and generalization to diverse narrative expressions. In this paper, we present MedVol-R1, a reinforcement learning-based framework for VRS that explicitly decouples evidence grounding from volumetric delineation: the LVLM grounds clinical reasoning to a verifiable 2D evidence anchor (key axial slice and 2D bounding boxes), which is then propagated into a coherent 3D mask by a frozen MedSAM2 module. We train MedVol-R1 with cold-start supervised fine-tuning followed by GRPO, guided by a multi-component reward that encourages informative evidence selection, accurate 2D spatial grounding, and cross-slice volumetric coherence, without requiring costly chain-of-thought annotations. Experiments on CT-ORG, AbdomenCT-1K, and KiTS23 from the M3D-Seg benchmark demonstrate that MedVol-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with reinforcement learning providing clear gains over pure supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [44] [DelowlightSplat: Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting for Lowlight 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26629)
*Fuzhen Jiang,Zengtian Xie,Zhuoran Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DelowlightSplat：一种针对低光照条件的轻量级前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，通过低光照适配器和基于代价体的多视角推理，从稀疏姿态图像中直接预测干净的3D高斯模型，实现清洁的新视角合成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人和AR/VR应用中，从稀疏姿态图像进行新视角合成和3D重建至关重要。然而，现有的前馈3D高斯重建方法在低光照条件下由于噪声、颜色偏移和不可靠的对应关系而失败。需要一种能够处理低光照条件的前馈高斯泼溅框架

Method: 1. 构建可控的多视角低光照基准：仅降级上下文视图而保持目标视图清洁；2. 引入轻量级低光照适配器进行残差增强以改善匹配性；3. 结合基于代价体的多视角推理直接预测干净的3D高斯模型

Result: 实验表明，DelowlightSplat在低光照条件下显著优于之前的前馈方法和两阶段流水线方法，能够实现清洁的新视角渲染

Conclusion: 提出的DelowlightSplat框架成功解决了低光照条件下前馈3D高斯重建的问题，通过低光照感知的架构实现了清洁的新视角渲染，为机器人和AR/VR应用中的低光照场景处理提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis and 3D reconstruction from sparse posed images are central to robotics and AR/VR. Yet, feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction fails under lowlight due to noise, color shifts, and unreliable correspondence. We propose DelowlightSplat, a lowlight-aware feed-forward Gaussian splatting framework for clean novel-view rendering. We build a controllable multi-view lowlight benchmark by degrading only context views while keeping target views clean. We introduce a lightweight Lowlight Adapter for residual enhancement to improve matchability, and couple it with cost-volume-based multi-view inference to directly predict clean 3D Gaussians. Experiments show that DelowlightSplat significantly outperforms previous feed-forward method and two-stage pipeline under lowlight conditions.

</details>


### [45] [JetViT: Efficient High-Resolution Vision Transformer with Post-Training Attention Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26636)
*Dongyun Zou,Zhuoyang Zhang,Junyu Chen,Wenkun He,Qinhe Peng,Hanrong Ye,Yao Lu,Hongxu Yin,Yu Wang,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JetViT是一种新型混合架构的视觉Transformer模型，通过后训练注意力搜索将全注意力ViT转换为高效混合注意力变体，在高分辨率图像上实现1.79倍吞吐量提升和44.81%延迟降低，且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全注意力视觉基础模型在处理高分辨率图像时计算效率低下，需要一种能在保持精度的同时大幅提升推理效率的方法。

Method: 提出后训练注意力搜索框架，将预训练的全注意力ViT转换为混合注意力变体：1)优化线性注意力块设计；2)寻找线性注意力和窗口注意力块的最佳组合；3)识别并保留关键的全注意力块。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上评估JetViT（基于DINOv3和DepthAnythingV2），达到1.79倍吞吐量提升和44.81%延迟降低，且精度不损失。

Conclusion: JetViT通过后训练架构转换实现了高效高分辨率视觉处理，为视觉基础模型的实用部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce JetViT, a novel family of hybrid-architecture Vision Transformer (ViT) models that match the accuracy of state-of-the-art full-attention vision foundation models while achieving substantially higher inference efficiency on high-resolution images. At the core of our approach is Post-Training Attention Search, a post-training acceleration framework that converts pre-trained full-attention ViTs into efficient hybrid-attention variants by identifying and replacing redundant full-attention blocks with linear or window-attention blocks. By inheriting the MLP and attention weights from the base model, Post-Training Attention Search efficiently explores the architectural design space through three key steps: (1) optimizing the linear-attention block design; (2) finding the best combination of linear-attention and window-attention blocks; and (3) identifying and preserving critical full-attention blocks. We evaluate JetViT on two representative high-resolution vision foundation models, DINOv3 and DepthAnythingV2. On the NVIDIA H100 GPU, JetViT achieves up to 1.79x higher throughput and up to 44.81% lower latency without sacrificing accuracy. We will release our code and accelerated ViT models soon.

</details>


### [46] [OmniRetriever: Any-to-Any Audio-Video-Text Retrieval via Fusion-as-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26641)
*Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Enmin Zhou,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniRetriever-7B通过融合教师蒸馏方法和Tuple-InfoNCE目标，解决了多模态编码器中三模态融合信号未被充分利用的问题，在多个零样本检索基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音频-视频-文本（AVT）编码器虽然能产生三模态联合嵌入，但标准的成对InfoNCE目标在训练时未能充分利用三模态同时可用的融合信号，存在改进空间。

Method: 提出融合教师蒸馏方法：将停止梯度的融合嵌入作为教师信号来监督单模态嵌入，同时引入Tuple-InfoNCE项直接监督融合嵌入。该方法实现为OmniRetriever-7B模型。

Result: 在六个零样本检索基准上，OmniRetriever-7B超越闭源Gemini Embedding 2（Clotho和SoundDescs上R@1提升13.3-18.0），在MSR-VTT和MSVD上达到当前视频-文本编码器的专业水平。在新发布的OmniRetriever-Bench（3782个三元组）上，平均分数34.84，比Gemini Embedding 2高1.72，比最佳开源AVT方法高8.03。

Conclusion: 融合教师蒸馏方法有效利用了多模态融合信号，OmniRetriever-7B在跨模态检索任务上取得了显著提升，展示了三模态联合表示的优势，并为多模态检索提供了新的基准和模型。

Abstract: Unified multimodal embedding spaces have become the standard interface for cross-modal retrieval and multimodal RAG, and recent audio-video-text (AVT) encoders extend this setting to three modalities. Such encoders can produce a joint (T,V,A) embedding whenever all three modalities are available, but standard pairwise InfoNCE objectives leave this signal unused during training. We close this gap with fusion-as-teacher distillation, which treats a stop-gradient copy of the fused embedding as a teacher signal for the single-modal embeddings, paired with a Tuple-InfoNCE term that supervises the fused embedding directly. We instantiate this objective as OmniRetriever-7B. Across six zero-shot retrieval benchmarks, OmniRetriever-7B surpasses the closed-source Gemini Embedding 2 by 13.3-18.0 R@1 on Clotho and SoundDescs, and reaches the contemporary zero-shot specialist band of open video-text encoders on MSR-VTT and MSVD. To stress-test joint representations, we further release OmniRetriever-Bench, a 12-direction AVT retrieval benchmark totaling 3782 triples; on it OmniRetriever-7B attains AVG-all 34.84, improving over Gemini Embedding 2 by 1.72 and over the best prior open-source AVT method by 8.03.

</details>


### [47] [Adaptation-Free Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception with Unseen Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26642)
*Hyunchul Bae,Heejin Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALF是一个协作感知框架，能够在未见过的智能体配置下实现零适配协作，通过将轻量级框级消息提升为与自车兼容的辅助特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协作感知方法大多假设固定或已知的协作方编码器配置，这在实际部署中受到限制。本文考虑开放世界场景，其中部署后可能出现配置未知的辅助智能体（如不同的激光雷达波束数量或编码器架构）。

Method: ALF通过将辅助智能体的框级消息转换为伪BEV地图，并结合目标中心线索与自车特征中的场景上下文，合成与自车兼容的潜在特征。

Result: 在V2X-Real数据集上，通过64个案例研究的零样本评估，ALF在相对mAP@0.7方面比先前最强基线提升了35.91%，同时每个智能体每帧仅需120字节（约9.6Kbps带宽，10Hz）。

Conclusion: ALF能够在开放世界设置中实现与未知配置智能体的零适配协作，显著提升3D目标检测性能，同时保持极低的通信带宽需求。

Abstract: Collaborative perception improves 3D object detection by enabling agents to share complementary observations, but most existing methods assume fixed or known collaborator encoder configurations, limiting deployment in practice. In this work, we consider an open-world setting in which auxiliary agents with unseen configurations may appear after deployment, such as different LiDAR beam counts or encoder architectures. To address this challenge, we propose ALF, a collaborative perception framework that enables zero-adaptation collaboration with unseen agent configurations by lifting lightweight box-level messages into ego-compatible auxiliary features. ALF converts auxiliary box-level messages into pseudo-BEV maps and synthesizes ego-compatible latent features by combining object-centric cues with scene context from the ego feature. On V2X-Real, under a zero-shot evaluation across 64 case studies, ALF outperforms the strongest prior baseline by 35.91% in relative mAP@0.7 while requiring only 120 bytes per agent per frame (approximately 9.6 Kbps bandwidth at 10 Hz).

</details>


### [48] [DV-SFT: Direct Vision Supervision for Fine-Grained Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26656)
*Jianfei Zhao,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Bing Wang,Zhixing Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DV-SFT提出了一种直接视觉监督的微调方法，通过为视觉token构建显式标签并使用与文本相同的下一个token预测目标来训练视觉token，从而增强MLLMs的细粒度视觉理解能力，无需修改模型架构或额外前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常在训练时只对文本token应用监督信号，而视觉token仅作为上下文的一部分被隐式优化，这导致视觉理解较为粗糙。先前工作尝试监督视觉输入但需要额外组件（如解码器或多轮前向传播），因为视觉token缺乏可直接解释的标签，限制了实际应用性。

Method: DV-SFT利用OCR相关场景中视觉-文本的直接对应关系，为每个视觉token自动标注其对应图像块中的单词，从而为视觉token构建显式的token级监督。该方法将MLLM视为黑盒，使用与文本相同的下一个token预测目标来训练视觉token，无需修改模型架构或额外前向传播。

Result: 在三个域内和四个域外基准测试中，DV-SFT始终优于标准SFT。进一步分析表明，视觉监督有效增强了细粒度视觉理解，并实现了更高的多模态对齐效率。

Conclusion: 直接视觉监督的微调方法DV-SFT通过为视觉token提供显式标签，显著提高了MLLMs的视觉理解能力，且保持了模型的黑盒特性，具有实际应用价值。该方法展示了为视觉token提供直接监督的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are typically trained end-to-end to predict ground-truth answers, yet supervision signals are applied exclusively to text tokens. Visual tokens, the core carriers of visual information, are optimized only implicitly as part of the context, leading to coarse-grained visual understanding. Prior works attempt to supervise visual inputs but inevitably rely on auxiliary components such as additional decoders or forward passes, because visual tokens lack readily interpretable labels. This limits their practical applicability. In this work, we propose \textbf{D}irect \textbf{V}ision \textbf{S}upervised \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning (DV-SFT), which constructs explicit, token-level supervision for visual tokens and trains them through the same next-token prediction objective used for text. Specifically, we exploit the direct vision--text correspondence in OCR-related scenarios and automatically label each visual token with the word in its corresponding image patch. DV-SFT treats the MLLM as a black box, requiring no architectural modifications or additional forward passes. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of direct vision supervision. DV-SFT consistently outperforms standard SFT across three in-domain and four out-of-domain benchmarks. Further analyses show that vision supervision effectively enhances fine-grained visual understanding and achieves higher multimodal alignment efficiency.

</details>


### [49] [Respecting Modality Gap in Post-hoc Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26661)
*Yuanwei Hu,Bo Peng,Yadan Luo,Zhen Fang,Ling Chen,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了使用文本嵌入作为类原型的现有零样本OOD检测范式，提出了一个在线伪监督框架来学习视觉特征空间中的类原型，以减少模态鸿沟并提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本OOD检测方法通常将类名的文本嵌入视为类原型，但本文从理论上证明了现成的文本原型与最优视觉原型存在不对齐问题，产生无法仅通过提示工程消除的固有模态鸿沟。为在后处理约束下缓解这一问题，需要开发新方法。

Method: 提出了一个在线伪监督框架，直接利用预训练视觉语言模型(VLMs)的软预测结果，从未标记的测试时数据流中学习视觉特征空间中的类原型。该方法在保持后处理约束的同时，通过在线优化过程学习更好的视觉原型。

Result: 理论分析为在线优化过程的收敛性提供了保证。大量实验证明，该方法在各种OOD检测设置下达到了新的最先进性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 文本作为原型的范式在零样本OOD检测中存在固有局限性，而直接在视觉特征空间中学习类原型能够更有效地减少模态鸿沟，为后处理零样本OOD检测提供了有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a popular technique to enhance the reliability of machine learning models by identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes. Recent progress in pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) has enabled zero-shot OOD detection without access to in-distribution (ID) training data; in this setting, existing methods commonly treat text embeddings of class names as class prototypes. In this paper, we challenge the widely adopted text-as-prototype paradigm by theoretically showing that off-the-shelf textual prototypes are generally misaligned with the optimal visual prototypes, yielding an intrinsic modality gap that cannot be eliminated by prompt engineering alone. To mitigate this gap under the post-hoc constraint, this paper presents an online pseudo-supervised framework that directly learns class prototypes in the visual feature space using unlabeled test-time data streams and soft predictions from the pre-trained VLMs. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of the online optimization procedure. Extensive experiments empirically demonstrate that our method achieves a new state of the art across a variety of OOD detection setups.

</details>


### [50] [DynFrame: Adaptive Reasoning-Driven Multimodal Framework with Dynamic Frame Augmentation for Complex Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26680)
*Peng Zhang,Guanghao Zhang,Wanggui He,Longxiang Zhang,Mushui Liu,Yan Xia,Zhenhao Peng,Weilong Dai,Jinlong Liu,Haobing Tang,Le Zhang,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynFrame是一个视频多模态大语言模型框架，通过可学习的时空密度检索和角色解耦强化学习优化，在单个自回归步骤中实现细粒度证据检索，显著提升视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频MLLM系统存在两个结构性缺陷：1）采样密度不可学习，导致细粒度证据需要多次检索调用，增加推理上下文长度和训练难度；2）检索和答案生成使用单一轨迹级优势函数优化，使"看哪里"和"如何回答"的token即使一个正确另一个错误也会获得相同信用。

Method: 提出DynFrame框架，在单个自回归过程中将时间窗口和采样密度作为原生token输出，实现可学习的跨度-密度检索。基于此，引入Segment-Decoupled GRPO（SD-GRPO），在检索边界分割每个rollout，并分配角色特定的token级优势，分别对采样决策和答案给予信用。

Result: 在DM-CoT-74k和DM-RL-45k数据集上训练，DynFrame-4B模型在六个基准测试（NExT-GQA、Charades-STA、ActivityNet-MR、Video-MME、MLVU、LVBench）上与强7B-8B基线模型竞争，而DynFrame-8B在大多数指标上创造了新的state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: DynFrame通过可学习的时空密度检索和角色解耦强化学习解决了现有视频MLLM系统的结构性缺陷，实现了更高效的多粒度证据获取和更精确的信用分配，显著提升了视频理解性能。

Abstract: Recent video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly couple step-by-step reasoning with on-demand visual evidence retrieval, allowing models to revisit relevant video segments during inference. However, two structural gaps remain in existing thinking-with-video systems. (i) Sampling density is not a learnable decision: existing methods may let the model decide where to look, but the per-window frame rate is largely fixed. As a result, fine-grained evidence is often recovered through repeated retrieval calls, which increases inference context length and training difficulty. (ii) Retrieval and answer generation are usually optimized with a single trajectory-level advantage, so the "where to look" tokens and the "how to answer" tokens receive the same credit even when one is correct and the other is not. To address these gaps, we present DynFrame, a framework that emits the temporal window and the sampling density as native tokens within a single autoregressive pass. This learnable span-density retrieval enables acquiring multi-granularity evidence with a single retrieval step. Based on the above tokenized retrieval interface, we further introduce Segment-Decoupled GRPO (SD-GRPO), which splits each rollout at the retrieval boundary and assigns role-specific token-level advantages, separately crediting the sampling decision and the answer. Trained on the curated DM-CoT-74k and DM-RL-45k, DynFrame-4B is competitive with strong 7B-8B baselines across six benchmarks (NExT-GQA, Charades-STA, ActivityNet-MR, Video-MME, MLVU, LVBench), and DynFrame-8B sets new state-of-the-art on most metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/zhangguanghao523/DynFrame.

</details>


### [51] [PinPoint: Prompting with Informative Interior Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26689)
*Pouya Sadeghi,Shawn He,Pedro Pablo Guerrero Vela,C. Thomas,Alex Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PinPoint是一个无需训练的点选择器，通过融合多种视觉线索生成共识图，选择边界外部的紧凑、空间多样点，在固定预算下用两个VLM调用实现与监督/RL调优方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型(VLM)和可提示分割器(SAM)的参考图像分割流水线中，VLM生成的边界框(bounding box)会让SAM需要猜测框内哪些像素属于目标对象。先前工作依赖的简单采样点常落在边界、干扰物或背景上，甚至可能比仅用边界框更差。监督和RL调优方法通过训练VLM预测更好的点来解决此问题，但作者认为这种训练是不必要的。

Method: 提出PinPoint方法：1) 融合四种视觉线索生成共识图；2) 选择紧凑、空间多样且远离边界的点；3) 使用冻结的VLM标记每个点。该方法完全无需训练，是确定性的点选择器，每个查询只需两个VLM调用。

Result: 在五个内部点的相同预算下，相比简单采样，PinPoint在RefCOCO/+/g数据集上将累积交并比(cIoU)提升12-18个点。无需任何任务特定训练，即能达到与监督和RL调优专业方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 性能差距主要来自提示模糊性，而非VLM的定位能力或SAM的容量。通过稳定、信息丰富的点选择替代简单采样，无需训练即可显著提升性能，证明了提示选择策略的重要性。

Abstract: Modern referring image segmentation pipelines couple a vision-language model (VLM) for grounding with a promptable segmenter such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for mask generation. Prior training-free instances of this recipe consistently trail fine-tuned and reinforcement-learning (RL)-tuned specialists, and it has been unclear whether the gap comes from the VLM's grounding, SAM's capacity, or the prompt. We show that the gap is dominated by prompt ambiguity: a VLM-proposed bounding box (bbox) leaves SAM to guess which pixels inside the bbox belong to the object the expression denotes. Interior points are the natural disambiguator, but where they fall matters; prior work relies on naively sampled points that land on boundaries, distractors, and background clutter, and can even hurt performance compared to the bbox alone. Supervised and RL-tuned methods close this gap by training a VLM to predict better points; we show that this training is unnecessary. At a matched budget of five interior points, replacing naive sampling with stable, informative point selection improves cumulative Intersection-over-Union (cIoU) by 12-18 points across RefCOCO/+/g, with every model fixed. We turn this observation into PinPoint, a deterministic, training-free point selector that fuses four visual cues into a consensus map, selects compact, spatially diverse points away from boundaries, and uses the frozen VLM to label each point. Without any task-specific training, PinPoint matches supervised and RL-tuned specialists on the same stack while issuing only two VLM calls per query.

</details>


### [52] [Rotation-Invariant Spherical Watermarking via Third-Order SO(3) Representation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26702)
*Pengzhen Chen,Yanwei Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Antonios Argyriou,Wu Liu,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于球面信号和SO(3)表示理论的旋转不变全景图像水印方法，通过高阶不可约表示的张量积构造三阶不变描述符（球面不变双谱），实现任意3D旋转下的鲁棒水印提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景图像的可靠水印面临任意3D旋转的根本挑战。传统平面表示和基于增强的鲁棒性策略在球面定义的全景图像上不适用且缺乏理论保证，因为全景图像在SO(3)群作用下自然变换。

Method: 将全景图像表述为球面信号，利用SO(3)表示理论推导可证明的旋转不变描述符。通过耦合高阶SO(3)不可约表示的张量积并投影到平凡表示，构造三阶不变描述符——球面不变双谱，该描述符保持相位信息且严格旋转不变。将水印嵌入高阶球面谐波系数，并通过不变双谱标量进行恢复。

Result: 提供了SO(3)不变性的理论证明，实验证明该方法对连续旋转具有近乎完美的鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉保真度。高阶不变构造克服了零阶统计的局限性，保留了方向信息并显著提升了嵌入容量。

Conclusion: 该方法通过严格的数学框架解决了全景图像水印的旋转不变性问题，为任意3D旋转下的可靠水印提取提供了理论保证和实用解决方案，在保持视觉质量的同时实现了卓越的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable watermarking of panoramic imagery is fundamentally challenged by arbitrary 3D rotations. As panoramas are defined on the sphere, they naturally transform under the action of $SO(3)$, rendering conventional planar representations and augmentation-based robustness strategies inadequate and devoid of theoretical guarantees. To address this, we formulate panoramas as spherical signals and leverage $SO(3)$ representation theory to derive provably rotation-invariant descriptors. While spherical harmonic coefficients transform equivariantly under rotations, the natural invariant constructions are typically limited to zeroth-order statistics which eliminate directional information and severely constrain embedding capacity. In this work, we introduce a principled third-order invariant construction by coupling higher-order $SO(3)$ irreducible representations via tensor products and projecting onto the trivial representation. This yields a spherical invariant bispectrum that preserves phase information while remaining strictly rotation-invariant. Leveraging this property, we embed watermarks into higher-order spherical harmonic coefficients and recover them from invariant bispectral scalars, enabling reliable extraction under arbitrary 3D rotations. We provide a theoretical proof of $SO(3)$ invariance for it and demonstrate experimentally its near-perfect robustness to continuous rotations while maintaining high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [53] [METATR: A Multilingual, Evolving Benchmark for Automatic Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26712)
*Mélodie Boillet,Solène Tarride,Christopher Kermorvant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: METATR (v1.0) 是一个多语言演进式基准测试，用于评估自动文本识别系统，特别是视觉大语言模型在多样化真实文档上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATR系统通常在包含现代英文印刷文本的数据集上评估，这限制了它们在许多实际应用中的相关性。真实文档具有多样性和复杂性，需要能够准确反映这些特性的代表性基准测试来选择合适的模型。

Method: METATR从多个公共收藏中收集多样化文档，涵盖29种语言、多种文字和版式。除了数据集本身，还定义了标准化的提示和归一化方法，建立了动态评估框架以确保结果可复现且具有扩展性。

Result: 评估了包括开源和闭源模型在内的多种最先进系统。结果显示，尽管专有模型性能最一致，但不同文字和版式之间的性能仍存在显著差异。

Conclusion: METATR提供了一个多维度的、面向实践者的框架，用于在真实条件下评估多语言ATR系统，并随着领域发展跟踪进展，促进有意义模型比较和选择。

Abstract: Benchmarks that reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world documents are essential for accurately evaluating Automatic Text Recognition (ATR) systems, especially Vision-Large Language Models (vLLMs). Although recent models demonstrate impressive performance, they are often evaluated on datasets containing modern, printed texts mostly written in English, which limits their relevance to many practical applications. Therefore, selecting a model for a specific use case requires evaluating it on data that matches the target documents. This highlights the importance of representative benchmarks for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce METATR (v1.0), a multilingual, evolving benchmark designed to evaluate ATR models across a wide range of documents, facilitating meaningful model comparison and selection. The benchmark was designed to maximize diversity by including documents from various public collections. These documents cover 29 languages and include texts with multiple scripts and layouts. Beyond the dataset itself, METATR defines a standardized prompting and normalization methodology and establishes a dynamic evaluation framework. This approach is intended to produce reproducible results while remaining extensible over time. We evaluated a wide range of state-of-the-art systems, including open-source models and closed-source models. Results are reported across various dimensions, including performance at the dataset and language levels, robustness to handwritten documents, and computational efficiency. Our findings show that, although proprietary models achieve the most consistent performance, substantial variability persists across scripts and layouts. Overall, METATR provides a multidimensional, practitioner-oriented framework for assessing multilingual ATR in real-world conditions and tracking progress as the field evolves.

</details>


### [54] [Joint 2D-3D Segmentation and Association in Street-level Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26725)
*Amir Melnikov,Masayuki Tanaka,Yusuke Monno,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的2D-3D联合分割与关联框架，用于街景图像解释，通过零样本检测和运动结构重建实现跨视角稳定对应，比传统2D跟踪方法性能提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 街景图像的准确解释对于大规模城市测绘和空间数字孪生环境创建至关重要。传统方法严重依赖时序帧进行跟踪，无法有效处理宽基线视角和变化的成像条件。

Method: 结合零样本检测与分割以及运动结构重建，建立稳定的跨视角对应关系。采用3D驱动的关联机制替代传统2D多目标跟踪，利用几何一致性指导跨视角的身份保持。

Result: 实验表明，与最先进的纯2D跟踪方法相比，该方法显著提高了对地面真值序列的覆盖度，实现了更鲁棒的身份保持，在具有挑战性的城市场景中获得了22%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过结合2D纹理线索和全局3D上下文，所提出的框架非常适合可扩展的街景处理，能够处理多种对象类型，为城市测绘和数字孪生提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate interpretation of street-level imagery is essential for large-scale urban mapping and the creation of Spatial Digital Twin (SDT) environments. This work presents a unified framework for joint 2D-3D segmentation and association that integrates visual semantics with multi-view geometric reasoning. Unlike conventional approaches that rely heavily on sequential frames for temporal tracking, our method leverages zero-shot detection and segmentation together with structure-from-motion reconstruction to establish stable cross-view correspondences. A 3D-driven association mechanism replaces traditional 2D multi-object tracking, using geometric consistency to guide identity preservation across wide-baseline viewpoints and varying imaging conditions. By combining 2D texture cues with global 3D context, the proposed pipeline is well-suited for scalable street-level processing and can be used for a variety of object types. Experiments demonstrate substantially improved coverage of ground-truth sequences and more robust identity retention compared to state-of-the-art 2D-only tracking methods, achieving a 22% performance gain in challenging urban scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [CIRCLED: A Multi-turn CIR Dataset with Consistent Dialogues across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26734)
*Tomohisa Takeda,Yu-Chieh Lin,Yuji Nozawa,Youyang Ng,Osamu Torii,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究人员构建了CIRCLED数据集，这是一个高质量的多轮组合图像检索基准，通过扩展三个现有数据集并添加对话历史一致性，超越了现有数据集在规模和通用性上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTCIR数据集存在两个主要问题：1) 缺乏对话历史一致性；2) 局限于时尚领域。这两个局限性阻碍了多轮组合图像检索研究的进展。

Method: 方法包括：1) 通过扩展FashionIQ、CIRR和CIRCO三个数据集构建CIRCLED；2) 使用基于CIReVL的检索管道生成数据；3) 应用多级过滤器（检索成功率、轮次长度、一致性、信息冗余）进行数据质量控制；4) 最终收集了22,608个多轮会话，涵盖9个子集。

Result: 构建了一个包含22,608个多轮会话的大规模数据集，显著超越了Multi-turn FashionIQ（11,505个会话）的规模和通用性。应用了多个基线方法对CIRCLED进行定量检索准确性评估，并公开了数据集和代码。

Conclusion: CIRCLED提供了一个实用、高质量的多轮组合图像检索基准，解决了现有数据集的局限性，将促进该领域的未来研究。数据集和代码已公开可用。

Abstract: Existing Multi-Turn Composed Image Retrieval (MTCIR) datasets lack dialogue-history consistency and are restricted to the fashion domain. To address these limitations, we construct CIRCLED by extending FashionIQ, CIRR, and CIRCO. In CIRCLED, the query at each turn progressively approaches the target image. Data are generated via a CIReVL-based retrieval pipeline and curated with multiple filters on retrieval success, turn length, consistency, and information redundancy to ensure quality. In total, we collect 22,608 multi-turn sessions across nine subsets, substantially exceeding Multi-turn FashionIQ (11,505 sessions) in both scale and generality. We further apply multiple baseline methods and quantitatively assess retrieval accuracy on CIRCLED. Our work provides a practical, high-quality benchmark to facilitate future research on multi-turn CIR. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tk1441/CIRCLED and https://github.com/mti-lab/circled.

</details>

### [56] [Self-Verified Distillation: Your Language Model Is Secretly Its Own Synthetic Data Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26132)
*Tony Lee,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Verified Distillation方法，仅使用无标签提示对预训练大语言模型进行后训练微调，通过自我验证过滤生成方案，在数学、科学和编程三个推理领域实现显著性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在仅有无标签种子问题、没有外部教师或工具反馈的情况下，后训练大语言模型是否能够仅通过无标签提示进行自我改进，解决当前需要大量人工标注或外部反馈的训练限制

Method: 提出Self-Verified Distillation算法：1) 模型为种子问题生成候选解决方案；2) 通过提示式自我验证进行过滤；3) 基于自筛选数据集进行训练。验证过程采用三级级联检查：循环一致性、事实性和正确性检查，要求所有验证阶段都通过才接受解决方案。通过增加候选生成数量和验证预算来提升数据质量

Result: 在Qwen3多尺度模型上验证该方法：Qwen3-4B在数学领域的综合pass@1提升16.7分（AIME26和HMMT），科学领域提升11.1分（GPQA Diamond和HLE），编程领域提升8.3分（LCBv5和LCBv6）。0.6B和8B模型也获得类似提升。相比仅测试时计算基线（UQ-TTC），在大多数设置下性能更优且仅需单次推理调用

Conclusion: Self-Verified Distillation证明了仅使用无标签提示进行后训练自我改进的可行性，通过自我验证过滤机制有效提升推理能力，为减少对人工标注或外部反馈依赖的模型自我完善提供了有效途径

Abstract: Can post-trained large language models (LLMs) further improve themselves using only unlabeled prompts, without external teachers or feedback from tools? We study this setting starting only from unlabeled seed questions with no ground-truth solutions, across three reasoning domains: math, science, and coding. We propose Self-Verified Distillation, a simple post-training refinement algorithm in which the model generates candidate solutions to these seed questions, filters them using prompt-based self-verification, and trains on the resulting self-curated dataset. Inspired by the UQ benchmark's use of multiple validators to screen candidate answers to hard unsolved questions, we adapt this validation-based filtering idea to self-training: the model filters its own generated solutions through a three-stage cascade of cycle-consistency, factuality, and correctness checks, accepting a solution only if it passes all stages with unanimous judge votes. We find that sampling more candidate generations and using a larger verification budget during training data construction produces higher-quality self-curated data and, in turn, better reasoning models. We then train Qwen3 models at multiple scales with Self-Verified Distillation and obtain gains across all three domains. For Qwen3-4B, our method improves aggregate held-out pass@1 by +16.7 points in math (AIME26 and HMMT), +11.1 points in science (GPQA Diamond and HLE), and +8.3 points in coding (LCBv5 and LCBv6), with gains also extending to 0.6B and 8B models. Compared to our test-time-only baseline (UQ-TTC), which improves performance by spending extra compute at inference time, Self-Verified Distillation achieves better performance in most settings while requiring only a single inference call at test time.

</details>


### [57] [Pretraining Data Exposure in Large Language Models: A Survey of Membership Inference, Data Contamination, and Security Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26133)
*Ziyi Tong,Feifei Sun,Le Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对LLM预训练数据暴露（PDE）问题进行了首次统一调研，将数据污染和成员推理两个相关但孤立研究领域整合在同一框架下，系统梳理了攻击与防御方法、实证发现及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模和预训练数据的增长，预训练数据暴露（PDE）问题日益凸显，这对评估完整性和隐私保护构成严重挑战。PDE涉及确定特定数据是否出现在LLM的预训练语料中，但相关研究（数据污染和成员推理）通常各自为政，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 1. 提出统一的PDE分析框架，在多个暴露水平上形式化PDE问题；2. 系统综述现有的攻击方法（检测数据是否在训练集中）和防御方法（保护训练数据隐私）；3. 综合实证研究发现；4. 识别研究空白和未来方向。

Result: 建立了PDE的统一理论框架，首次将数据污染和成员推理整合在同一问题视域下。系统梳理了两大领域的攻击与防御技术，总结了关键实证发现（如不同因素对检测效果的影响），揭示了当前研究中的局限性和不一致性。

Conclusion: PDE是LLM安全和隐私评估的核心问题，需要跨领域协作解决。统一框架有助于推动该领域的系统性研究，但仍面临检测精度、防御有效性和评估标准化等挑战，需要更鲁棒的解决方案和标准化基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the predominant paradigm in NLP, advancing both research and industry. As model sizes and pretraining data grow, concerns about Pretraining Data Exposure (PDE) increase due to the scale and opacity of training datasets.
  PDE refers to determining whether specific data appeared in an LLM's pretraining corpus. It is critical for ensuring evaluation integrity and protecting privacy, intersecting two key areas: data contamination and membership inference. Though conceptually related, these areas have often been studied in isolation. This paper offers the first unified survey of both under the PDE framework. We formalize PDE across exposure levels, review attack and defense methods, synthesize empirical findings, and highlight open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [58] [CroCo: Cross-Lingual Contrastive Preference Tuning on Self-Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26293)
*Mike Zhang,Ali Basirat,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 跨语言对比偏好调优方法（CroCo）能够在无需语言特定偏好标注的情况下，通过英语训练的奖励模型在多语言环境中实现有效的偏好优化，在14种高/低资源语言上均展现出良好性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，基于大型语言模型自生成回复的受控对比性（通过奖励分数设置）能够改进英语环境的下游偏好调优。本研究旨在将这一方法扩展到多语言环境，探索在缺乏语言特定偏好标注的情况下，跨语言偏好优化的可行性。

Method: 提出跨语言对比偏好调优（CroCo）方法：1）使用在英语偏好数据上训练的奖励模型（基于多语言基础模型）对多种语言的回复进行排名；2）在单语言或多语言设置中进行配对优化；3）强调使用策略内（on-policy）数据而非策略外（off-policy）数据的重要性。

Result: 在14种高/低资源语言的评估中：1）EuroLLM-9B在结构化任务上于6/7语言中匹配或超越基础模型；2）Aya-3B在4/7设置中表现优于基础模型；3）在开放生成任务中，两个调优模型在所有11种评估语言中均优于各自的基础模型；4）策略外回复会降低收益，在线偏好优化未能超越离线变体。

Conclusion: 跨语言对比偏好调优（CroCo）能够在无需语言特定偏好标注的情况下实现有效的多语言偏好优化，避免了监督微调的灾难性遗忘，为多语言偏好调优提供了有前景的方向。关键在于使用策略内数据和离线优化方法。

Abstract: Prior work establishes that controlled contrastiveness between self-generated responses from large language models, set via reward scores, improves downstream preference tuning in English. We extend this method to multiple languages and evaluate two models across a total of 14 high and low-resource languages on a diverse set of tasks. Our central finding is that cross-lingual contrastive preference tuning on self-generations (CroCo) transfers without language-specific preference annotation. A reward model trained on English preferences (atop a multilingual base) produces useful within-language rankings across most languages, and pairing in either a monolingual or multilingual setting improves over each model on the majority of setups while preventing the catastrophic forgetting of supervised fine-tuning. We observe that the gains require on-policy data. Off-policy responses reduce the benefit and online preference optimization fails to improve over the offline variant. Specifically, on structured tasks, our method matches or exceeds the base in 6/7 languages for EuroLLM-9B and 4/7 settings for Aya-3B. On open-ended generation, both tuned models win against their respective base across 11 evaluated languages. Overall, we show promising directions for multilingual preference tuning.

</details>


### [59] [The Daily Dose: Workflow-Integrated Large Language Model Automation for Clinical Summarization and Trial Identification in Radiation Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26346)
*Jason Holmes,Federico Mastroleo,Mariana Borras-Osorio,Srinivas Seetamsetty,Satomi Shiraishi,Mirek Fatyga,Judy C. Boughey,Cornelius A. Thiels,William G. Breen,Daniel J. Ma,Daniel K. Ebner,David M. Routman,Brady S. Laughlin,Carlos E. Vargas,Samir H. Patel,Sujay A. Vora,Nadia N. Laack,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Wei Liu,Mark R. Waddle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究开发并评估了"The Daily Dose"（TDD）系统，这是一个基于大语言模型的自动化临床摘要和临床试验识别系统，用于放疗肿瘤学实践。通过混合方法评估显示系统具有高接受度和时间节省效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗肿瘤学实践中，临床医生需要处理大量患者信息并识别相关临床试验，这一过程耗时且可能不全面。需要开发自动化系统来简化临床信息汇总和试验匹配流程，提高工作效率。

Method: 采用混合方法评估，包括横断面匿名临床医生调查。在系统部署1个月后，55名参与者完成调查。使用RadOnc-GPT生成医生特定电子邮件摘要，包括患者日程安排、基于电子健康记录的临床状态摘要，以及为新患者或咨询就诊自动识别相关临床试验。

Result: 94.5%的受访者在放疗肿瘤学领域工作，69.1%为主治医师。83.6%参与者每天或每周多次使用TDD。在5点Likert量表上，可用性和满意度平均得分为3.89，感知有用性为3.43，影响和未来使用意愿为3.80。27%参与者估计每天节省≥10分钟时间，总体满意度与感知时间节省显著正相关。问卷显示出优异的内在一致性（Cronbach's α = 0.97）。

Conclusion: TDD系统在放疗肿瘤学实践中表现出良好的接受度和实用性，能够节省临床医生时间并提高工作效率。系统的高度内在一致性表明测量工具可靠。这些积极结果为在肿瘤学和其他专科中扩展使用基于大语言模型的临床摘要系统提供了支持。

Abstract: Objective: To describe the design and early clinical evaluation of The Daily Dose (TDD), an LLM-driven, automated clinical summarization and clinical-trial identification system integrated into routine radiation oncology practice. Design: Mixed-methods evaluation using a cross-sectional, anonymous clinician survey administered after 1 month of system deployment. Exposure: Daily automated delivery of physician-specific email summaries generated using RadOnc-GPT, including patient schedules, concise EHR-derived clinical-status summaries, and automated identification of potentially relevant clinical trials for new or consult visits. Main Outcomes and Measures: Primary outcomes included self-reported usability, satisfaction, perceived usefulness, perceived impact on workflow, time savings, and intention for continued use. Internal consistency reliability was assessed using Cronbach's $α$. Results: Among 55 respondents, 52 (94.5\%) worked in radiation oncology, and 38 (69.1\%) were attending physicians. Most participants (83.6\%) reported using TDD daily or several times per week. Mean (SD) scores were 3.89 (1.04) for usability and satisfaction, 3.43 (1.24) for perceived usefulness, and 3.80 (1.17) for impact and future use (5-point Likert scale). Overall satisfaction was positively associated with perceived time savings ($p < .001$). Participants reported variable time savings, with 27\% estimating $\geq 10$ minutes saved per day. The questionnaire demonstrated excellent internal consistency (overall Cronbach's $α$ = 0.97).

</details>


### [60] [Why LLMs Hallucinate on Structured Knowledge: A Mechanistic Analysis of Reasoning over Linearized Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26362)
*Shanghao Li,Jinda Han,Yibo Wang,Yuanjie Zhu,Zihe Song,Langzhou He,Kenan Kamel A Alghythee,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了LLMs在结构化知识推理任务中产生幻觉的机制：注意力过度集中于结构性线索而非完整上下文，前馈表示未能将外部知识有效grounding导致模型回归到参数记忆，这些系统性内部动态而非随机噪声导致幻觉发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在处理结构化外部知识（如图形、表格）时能获取充分信息，但仍会产生幻觉输出。目前对这种失败背后的机制了解不足，因此需要深入探究幻觉产生的内部动态机制。

Method: 通过分析LLMs的内部机制，研究注意力分布模式和前馈层表示的grounding能力。发现注意力过度集中到类似捷径的结构线索而非完整上下文。进一步探究语义grounding失败与参数记忆回归之间的关系。将发现扩展到多跳图和表格设置中进行验证。

Result: 1. 幻觉源于系统性内部动态而非随机噪声；2. 注意力不平衡地集中在结构性线索上；3. 前馈表示无法有效grounding外部知识，导致模型回归参数记忆；4. 幻觉与语义grounding失败一致相关，而注意力分配表现出更大的任务依赖性；5. 这些机制模式从单跳图推广到多跳和表格设置，支持跨结构化知识格式的有效幻觉检测。

Conclusion: LLMs在结构化知识推理中的幻觉主要由系统性机制缺陷引起：注意力偏置和前馈层grounding失败。这些发现为理解模型幻觉提供了新的机制视角，并支持开发更有效的幻觉检测方法，特别是对于依赖结构化知识的推理任务。

Abstract: In many reasoning tasks, large language models (LLMs) rely on structured external knowledge, such as graphs and tables, which is typically linearized into sequential token representations. However, even when sufficient knowledge is available, LLMs can still produce hallucinated outputs, and the underlying mechanisms behind such failures remain poorly understood. We investigate these mechanisms and find that hallucinations arise from systematic internal dynamics rather than random noise. First, attention disproportionately concentrates toward shortcut-like structural cues rather than distributing across the full context. Second, feed-forward representations fail to ground the provided knowledge, causing the model to revert to parametric memory. Moreover, our results indicate that hallucination is consistently associated with failures in semantic grounding within feed-forward layers, while attention allocation exhibits greater task-dependent variability. Finally, we show that these mechanistic patterns generalize beyond single-hop graphs to multi-hop and tabular settings, enabling effective hallucination detection across structured knowledge formats.

</details>


### [61] [Cultural Value Alignment Via Latent Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26365)
*Trung Duc Anh Dang,Sarah Masud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于文化评估和干预的通用框架，通过从抽象查询转向基于情境的行为探测，绕过表层对齐来映射LLMs的潜在文化价值观坐标，并引入激活导向技术在不重新训练的情况下调整内部对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）常表现出同质化的文化视角，传统的基于世界价值观调查（WVS）的直接提示方法无法有效获取模型潜在的文化深度，通常导致安全对齐拒绝或中性响应。

Method: 1. 从抽象查询过渡到基于情境的行为探测框架；2. 通过提取300个情境困境中的隐式标记概率来绕过表层对齐；3. 引入激活导向技术在前向传播过程中调整内部对齐而无需重新训练。

Result: 发现多个LLMs在适应性方面存在显著差异，并揭示了潜在纠缠现象——沿一个文化维度的干预会引起另一个维度的偏移，表明文化价值观作为耦合结构编码存在，限制了精确对齐。

Conclusion: 建立了一个计算高效的文化导向框架，突出了在通过LLMs导航全球价值观时面临的结构复杂性，文化价值观作为耦合结构编码的特性限制了精确对齐的可能性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often exhibit homogenized cultural perspectives. While the World Values Survey (WVS) provides a gold standard for mapping human values, traditional direct prompting of LLMs on WVS often fails to access the model's latent cultural depth, leading to safety-aligned refusals or neutral responses. Here, we propose a generalizable framework for cultural evaluation and intervention that transitions from abstract queries to scenario-based behavioral probing. By extracting implicit token probabilities across 300 situational dilemmas, we bypass surface-level alignment to map the latent coordinates of LLMs cultural value. We further introduce activation steering to shift these internal alignments during the forward pass without retraining. Across multiple LLMs, we find substantial variation in adaptability and uncover a consistent phenomenon of latent entanglement, where interventions along one cultural dimension induce shifts along another. These results suggest that cultural values are encoded as coupled structures, limiting precise alignment. This work establishes a computationally efficient framework for cultural steering, highlighting the structural complexities when navigating global value with LLMs.

</details>


### [62] [Memory Architectures for Multi-Turn Text-to-SQL: A Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26394)
*Ravi Kumar Tummalapenta,Suman Addanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了EnterpriseMem-Bench基准测试，用于评估企业级多轮Text-to-SQL任务的记忆能力，发现无状态模型性能急剧下降，工作记忆主导效果，并观察到Claude模型存在代际退化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析中的多轮Text-to-SQL主要被评估为单轮任务，缺乏对其记忆能力的系统性评估。现有研究在多轮对话中记忆机制对SQL生成准确性的影响方面存在空白。

Method: 构建了EnterpriseMem-Bench基准测试，包含300个会话和1400轮对话，涵盖三个企业领域。评估了5个前沿模型在5种记忆条件下的表现，使用Memory Benefit Score作为诊断指标，并通过三因素消融实验分离工作记忆窗口大小、情景检索和语义增强的效果。

Result: 四个主要发现：1) 无状态多轮Text-to-SQL在第3轮执行准确率降至零；2) 记忆架构复杂性非单调改善准确率，工作记忆主导效果；3) Claude Sonnet 4.6比4.5在SEC EDGAR上表现差17-33个百分点；4) 在推理模式下，Claude错误分布变为单模态——每个错误都是结果错误。

Conclusion: 工作记忆是多轮Text-to-SQL的关键组件，更复杂的记忆架构不一定带来更好性能。Claude模型的代际退化问题需要关注，为社区提供了新的基准测试和评估框架。

Abstract: Multi-turn Text-to-SQL is central to enterprise analytics yet remains predominantly evaluated in single-turn settings. We introduce EnterpriseMem-Bench, a multi-turn Text-to-SQL benchmark of 300 sessions and 1,400 turns built programmatically from three enterprise domains (BIRD financial, SEC EDGAR, Northwind), with deterministic ground truth and per-turn memory-critical annotation. We evaluate five frontier models -- GPT-5 mini, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, and Opus 4.6 -- across five memory conditions enabling a three-way ablation isolating working-memory window size, episodic retrieval, and semantic augmentation as independent effects. All Claude models are evaluated with extended thinking enabled to maintain parity with GPT reasoning models. We introduce the Memory Benefit Score (MBS) as a per-turn diagnostic metric. Four findings emerge: (1) stateless multi-turn Text-to-SQL collapses to zero execution accuracy by Turn 3 across all five models, even under reasoning; (2) memory-architecture complexity does not monotonically improve accuracy -- working memory dominates, and additional components produce model- and dataset-dependent effects from +14 to -16 percentage points; (3) Claude Sonnet 4.6 underperforms Sonnet 4.5 by 17-33pp on SEC EDGAR across conditions, a generational regression persisting under reasoning; (4) under reasoning, Claude error distributions become mono-modal -- every non-correct turn is a wrong-result error. We release the benchmark, agent, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [63] [Annotator Positionality as Signal: Psychometric Weighting for Anti-Autistic Ableism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26397)
*Naba Rizvi,Harper Strickland,Saleha Ahmedi,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一个针对反自闭症能力主义语言的偏见感知评估框架，发现大型语言模型（LLMs）经常产生有害输出，错误标记社区重新定义的术语，并在评估工具被屏蔽时表现出对自闭症人士更负面的态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于决策任务，可能会放大或压制某些观点，这在影响自闭症社区的高风险环境中引发担忧。虽然先前研究已识别出LLMs中的残疾相关偏见，但尚不清楚它们如何概念化能力主义或检测文本中的能力主义。

Method: 引入了一个偏见感知评估框架，该框架针对反自闭症能力主义语言，采用心理测量加权、社区接近的真实标准，以注释者的立场性为基础。这一框架比传统的多数投票聚合标准更严格，因为后者显著且持续地低估了自闭症和自闭症接纳观点。

Result: 研究发现LLMs经常产生有害输出，错误地将社区重新定义的语言标记为能力主义，当评估工具被屏蔽时，对自闭症人士表现出更负面的态度。错误分析显示模型依赖表面关键词匹配而非上下文因素（如说话者身份、语言是否促进群体内团结或造成群体外伤害）。

Conclusion: 当前的LLMs在理解和检测针对自闭症的能力主义语言方面存在严重缺陷，特别是缺乏考虑上下文和社会语言学因素的能力。需要开发更敏感的评估框架来捕捉边缘化社区的观点，避免偏见传播。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in decision-making tasks where they can amplify or suppress perspectives, raising concerns in high-stakes settings affecting autistic communities. While previous research has identified disability-related biases in LLMs, it remains unclear how they conceptualize ableism or detect it in text. We introduce a bias-aware evaluation framework targeting anti-autistic ableist language with a psychometrically-weighted, community-proximate ground truth anchored in annotator positionality. This framework constitutes a stricter standard than conventional majority-vote aggregation which significantly and consistently underweights autistic and autism-accepting perspectives. We find that LLMs frequently produce harmful outputs, mislabel community-reclaimed language as ableist, and express more negative attitudes toward autistic people when assessment instruments are masked. Our error analysis reveals that models rely on surface-level keyword matching rather than contextual factors such as speaker identity, and whether the language fosters in-group solidarity or inflicts out-group harm.

</details>


### [64] [Towards Just-in-Time Adaptive Feedback: Enhancing Student Learning via Knowledge-Grounded LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26405)
*Younghun Lee,Amir Bralin,Nobel Sanjay Rebello,Dan Goldwasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大型语言模型的即时反馈框架，通过将领域专家知识融入LLM，为学生提供自适应反馈，在大型大学课程中显著提升学生学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型能够大规模生成自适应反馈，但目前的研究缺乏在真实教学环境中提供即时反馈的清晰方法。需要一种能够将领域专业知识与LLM结合，在真实教学场景中提供有效即时反馈的系统化框架。

Method: 提出一个框架：收集学生的书面推理逻辑（策略论文），基于推理内容分析潜在错误类型，提供非侵入性反馈以澄清缺失或错误概念。将领域专家知识融入LLM，实现自适应即时反馈。

Result: 该框架在大型大学课程中部署（N > 1000），相比之前学期提高了80%以上的学生表现。通过分析学习轨迹验证了教学效用，展示了与LLM的迭代对话如何促进错误观念向正确理解的转变。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的即时反馈框架通过融入领域专家知识，能够有效提升大规模教学环境中的学生学习表现，并为错误观念的修正提供系统化支持，展示了人工智能增强教学的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Educational interventions are effective tools for enhancing student learning. While Large Language Models (LLMs) allow for generating adaptive feedback at scale, current studies lack clear methodologies for providing Just-in-Time (JiT) feedback in authentic instructional settings. In this paper, we present a framework that provides adaptive feedback by grounding LLMs with domain-specific expert knowledge. Our approach collects written reasoning logic (strategy essays) from students, analyzes potential error types based on the content of that reasoning, and delivers non-intrusive feedback designed to clarify missing or incorrect concepts. We deploy this framework in a large-scale university course (N > 1000), where it improved student performance by over 80% compared to previous semesters. Lastly, we validate the framework's pedagogical utility by analyzing the learning trajectories; we demonstrate how iterative conversations with LLM facilitate shifting one's misconception to correct understanding.

</details>


### [65] [Probing Minimalist Phase Structure in LLMs: What Universal Dependencies Cannot Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26431)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过设计UD距离不变但相位数变化的wh移位刺激，发现LLMs编码了形式句法抽象（如相位边界和相位内聚性），超越UD标注的局限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用依存关系（UD）不编码形式句法抽象如相位边界或相位内聚性，现有基于UD的结构探针无法确定大语言模型是否编码这些抽象。需要设计能够超越UD限制的评估方法来探究LLMs中的形式句法表示。

Method: 设计了三种wh移位刺激条件（简单小句、不定式和限定式），这些条件按wh元素穿越的句法相位边界数量排序，但UD距离在各条件中保持相同。在13个LLMs（来自四个模型家族）上测试结构探针，使用激活修补来验证表示的因果活性。

Result: 1) 在12/13模型中观察到跨小句配对的相位计数梯度效应；2) 在13/13模型中观察到小句内配对的符号不对称性（UD距离相同但行为不同）；3) 激活修补确认12/13模型中的这些表示具有因果活性。

Conclusion: 分布预训练能够诱导出与形式句法抽象对齐的表示，这些抽象超出了基于标注的探针所能触及的范围。基于UD的探针仅为句法编码提供了下限而非上限，LLMs确实编码了UD之外的形式句法结构。

Abstract: Structural probes train on Universal Dependencies (UD), which does not encode formal-syntactic abstractions such as phase boundaries or phase-internal cohesion. Whether large language models (LLMs) encode these remains an open question that UD-based probing cannot answer by construction. We evaluate structural probes on wh-movement stimuli where UD distances are invariant across conditions by design -- any non-zero effect therefore reflects structure beyond UD. The three conditions -- bare small clause, infinitival, and finite -- are ordered by the number of Minimalist Program (MP) phase boundaries the wh-element crosses.
  Across 13 LLMs from four families, we find a phase-count gradient on a cross-clause pair (12/13 models) and a 13/13 sign asymmetry on a within-clause pair whose UD distance is identical across conditions -- the latter specifically predicted by phase-internal cohesion, an MP abstraction invisible to UD by construction. Activation patching confirms the representations are causally active in 12/13 models. These findings suggest that distributional pretraining can induce representations aligned with formal-syntactic abstractions beyond the reach of annotation-based probing; UD-grounded probes provide a lower bound on syntactic encoding, not an upper bound.

</details>


### [66] [Vectors Are Not Neutral: Sensitive-Information Inference from Exported LLM Representations in Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26433)
*Weixin Liu,Bowen Qu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了大型语言模型(LLM)摘要系统中紧凑向量表示可能导致的隐私泄露风险，特别是在临床出院总结生成场景下，使用电子健康记录中的种族信息作为敏感标签进行审计，并提出了SurfaceLoRA方法来减少特定向量中的敏感信息可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM摘要系统会将输入文档的紧凑向量表示传递给下游的检索、监控、审计或分析工作流。即使源文档保持访问限制，衍生的向量可能在不同访问控制下处理，仍然支持敏感信息推断，造成剩余信息泄露风险。临床出院总结生成作为高风险案例研究，需要解决这种隐私泄露问题。

Method: 使用电子健康记录(EHR)中的种族作为受控敏感标签进行审计。审计系统可能保留或暴露给下游组件的两种向量：最终提示令牌隐藏状态和平均池化提示表示。作为缓解方法，提出了SurfaceLoRA——一种针对导出向量的参数高效微调方法，使用梯度反转判别器附加到指定导出向量。

Result: 研究显示，从一个导出向量减少敏感标签的可恢复性并不一定从另一个向量中减少。在平衡的五类探测协议下，SurfaceLoRA将目标最终令牌向量中的EHR记录种族可恢复性降低到接近随机水平，同时保持摘要效用，但未针对的池化向量中的可恢复性仍然显著较高。

Conclusion: 隐私审计和缓解措施应在确切保留或暴露给下游组件的向量上进行，因为不同向量表示中的敏感信息可恢复性可能不同，针对特定向量的缓解措施可能对其他向量无效。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) summarization systems may pass compact vector representations of private inputs to downstream retrieval, monitoring, audit, or analytic workflows. Even when source documents remain access-restricted, derived vectors may be handled under different access controls and still support sensitive-information inference, creating a residual information-disclosure risk. We study this issue in clinical discharge-summary generation as a high-stakes case study, using electronic health record (EHR)-recorded race as a controlled sensitive-label audit. We audit two artifacts that a system might retain or expose to downstream components: the final prompt-token hidden state and the mean-pooled prompt representation. Our results show that reducing recoverability of the case-study sensitive label from one exported artifact does not necessarily reduce recoverability from another. As a mitigation case study, we introduce SurfaceLoRA, an exported-vector-targeted parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that uses a gradient-reversal discriminator attached to a designated exported vector. Under a balanced five-way probing protocol, SurfaceLoRA reduces EHR-recorded race recoverability from the targeted final-token artifact toward chance while preserving summarization utility, yet recoverability remains substantially higher from untargeted pooled artifacts. These findings show that privacy auditing and mitigation should be performed on the exact vector artifact retained or exposed to downstream components.

</details>


### [67] [Targeted Remasking: Replacing Token Editing with Token-to-Mask Refinement in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26436)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的Token-to-Mask（T2M）remasking方法来改进离散掩码扩散语言模型的生成过程，替代原有的Token-to-Token（T2T）编辑机制，通过在推理过程中将疑似错误的已确定token重新恢复为mask状态，让扩散过程在更干净的上下文中重新预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLaDA2.1中的Token-to-Token（T2T）编辑机制存在三个基本局限性：1）将错误检测与替换耦合在一起；2）用可能错误的token污染生成上下文；3）引入训练-推理噪声不匹配，因为模型生成的系统性错误与训练时看到的随机扰动不同。

Method: 提出了Token-to-Mask（T2M）remasking方法，这是一种无需训练的直接替代方案。设计并验证了三种互补的错误检测策略：概率基检测、触发镜像检测和时差基检测。该方法将疑似错误的已提交token重置为mask状态，让扩散过程在更干净的上下文中重新预测。

Result: 在12个基准测试（涵盖知识、推理、数学、编程和指令遵循）上的综合实验表明，T2M通常在需要精确token级输出的任务上提升性能，其中数学任务上提升最大（CMATH上+5.92%）。在CMATH上的错误分析显示，主要失败模式是"最后一公里token损坏"，T2M修复了其中59.4%的此类情况。

Conclusion: T2M remasking通过净化生成上下文、将系统性推理错误转换回模型原生的掩码噪声类型，以及实现延迟承诺以进行联合多位置优化，提供了理论上的优势。该方法为离散掩码扩散语言模型的生成过程提供了有效的改进方案，特别是在需要精确输出的任务上表现显著。

Abstract: Discrete masked diffusion language models such as LLaDA generate text through iterative denoising, where mask tokens are progressively replaced with predicted tokens. LLaDA2.1 introduced a Token-to-Token (T2T) editing mechanism that accelerates generation by directly replacing committed tokens suspected of being incorrect. However, we identify fundamental limitations of T2T editing: it couples error detection with replacement, pollutes the generation context with potentially incorrect tokens, and introduces a train-inference noise mismatch where systematic model-generated errors differ from the random perturbations seen during training. We propose Token-to-Mask (T2M) remasking, a training-free, drop-in replacement for T2T editing that resets suspected erroneous tokens back to the mask state, allowing the diffusion process to re-predict them under cleaner context. We design and empirically validate three complementary error detection strategies -- probability-based, trigger-mirrored, and temporal-difference-based -- and provide a unified theoretical analysis showing that T2M remasking purifies the generation context, converts systematic inference errors back to the model's native mask noise type, and enables delayed commitment for joint multi-position optimization. Comprehensive experiments across 12 benchmarks spanning knowledge, reasoning, mathematics, coding, and instruction following show that T2M generally improves performance on tasks requiring precise token-level output, with the largest gain on mathematics (+5.92% on CMATH). Error analysis on CMATH reveals that the dominant failure mode is last-mile token corruption -- where correct reasoning produces a corrupted final answer -- and that T2M repairs 59.4% of such cases.

</details>


### [68] [LURE: Live-Usage Replay Evaluations for Reducing Evaluation Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26438)
*Igor Ivanov,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LURE（实时使用回放评估）是一种通过回放真实的智能体交互轨迹并在末尾附加评估提示来构建部署式评估的方法，解决了大语言模型在评估时的"评估意识"问题，提升了安全和对齐基准的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够识别何时正在被评估（评估意识），并因此表现出不同的行为，这破坏了安全和对齐基准的有效性。现有的评估方法与实际部署场景存在差异，需要更贴近真实使用情况的评估方法。

Method: 提出了LURE方法：通过回放真实的智能体交互轨迹并在末尾附加评估提示来构建部署式评估。同时引入自动化管道来测量评估的现实性，结合评估意识言语化检测和法官模型对日志是否为评估的概率估计，并在大量部署和评估转录数据集上进行验证。

Result: LURE-based评估与部署情况的区分度显著低于广泛使用的基准和合成评估生成器，可以接近真实用户对话的现实性。该方法已在阴谋、AI安全破坏和谄媚设置中实例化。

Conclusion: 评估现实性是对齐基准的关键属性，应与基准结果一起报告，特别是在这些结果用于安全案例时。LURE为构建更贴近实际部署的评估提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Large language models can recognize when they are being evaluated (evaluation awareness) and behave differently because of that, which undermines the validity of safety and alignment benchmarks. We propose LURE (Live-Usage Replay Evaluations), a method for constructing deployment-like evaluations by replaying realistic agentic interaction trajectories and appending evaluation prompt at the end. We also introduce an automated pipeline for measuring evaluation realism, combining detection of verbalized evaluation awareness and judge-model estimates of the probability of logs being an evaluation, and validate it on a large dataset of deployment and evaluation transcripts. We find that LURE-based evaluations are substantially less distinguishable from deployment than widely used benchmarks and synthetic evaluation generators, and can approach the realism of real conversations with users. We instantiate LURE in scheming, AI safety sabotage, and sycophancy settings. Our results suggest that evaluation realism is a crucial property of alignment benchmarks and should be reported alongside benchmark results, especially when such results are used in safety cases.

</details>


### [69] [Conv-to-Bench: Evaluating Language Models Via User-Assistant Dialogues In Code Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26440)
*Victor M. dos Santos,Andre C. Castro,Samuel L. de S. Toledo,Bruno M. L. Calura,Lisandra C. de M. Menezes,Raul C. R. Mata,Telma W. de L. Soares,Bryan L. M. de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Conv-to-Bench是一个多阶段框架，能够自动将真实的多轮用户-助手对话转换为结构化、可验证的需求检查表，用于解决传统LLM评估基准难以扩展的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型快速发展已超过传统评估基准的可扩展性，传统基准依赖劳动密集型的专家策划，这已成为瓶颈。需要一种能自动从真实对话中提取评估标准的方法。

Method: Conv-to-Bench是一个多阶段框架，利用真实对话日志中的"指令演化"现象，将碎片化的用户意图解构为整合指令和二进制评估标准。在编程领域应用该方法，通过指令中心提取创建评估集。

Result: Conv-to-Bench生成的评估集与人类撰写标准(如BigCodeBench)近乎完美对齐，Spearman相关系数高达ρ=1.000，计算开销显著降低。主要评估器与人工验证真值达成实质性一致(κ=0.705)。消融研究表明多轮交互能捕捉用户意图的迭代演化，而指令中心提取提供更稳健的基础。

Conclusion: Conv-to-Bench提供了一个可扩展、成本效益高的范式，能够随着以用户为中心的AI应用多样化而保持高保真度评估标准，有效解决了LLM评估基准的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has outpaced the scalability of traditional evaluation benchmarks, which remain heavily dependent on labor-intensive expert curation. We address this bottleneck with Conv-to-Bench, a multi-stage framework that automatically transforms authentic multi-turn user-assistant dialogues into structured, verifiable requirement checklists. By leveraging the "instructional evolution" found in real-world conversational logs, our approach deconstructs fragmented user intent into consolidated instructions and binary evaluation criteria. Applied to the programming domain, Conv-to-Bench produces evaluation sets that demonstrate near-perfect alignment with human-authored standards like BigCodeBench, achieving Spearman correlations of up to $ρ$ = 1.000 with significantly lower computational overhead. Validation of the LLM-as-a-judge framework further confirms its reliability, with the primary evaluator achieving substantial agreement with human-verified ground truth ($κ$ = 0.705). Our comprehensive ablation studies reveal that while multi-turn interactions capture the iterative evolution of user intent, instruction-centric extraction provides a more robust foundation. Ultimately, Conv-to-Bench provides a scalable, cost-effective paradigm for maintaining high-fidelity evaluation standards as user-centric AI applications continue to diversify.

</details>


### [70] [Alignment Tuning for Large Language Models: A Data-Centric Lens on Alignment Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26442)
*Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从数据中心的视角重新审视对齐调整，将其构建为管道设计问题，将现有对齐方法纳入统一分类体系，并识别设计权衡和原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐调整文献主要围绕优化目标展开，而对齐数据的构建往往被隐式处理。本文旨在通过数据中心视角重新组织对齐调整方法。

Method: 将对齐数据构建分解为三个交互阶段：响应合成、偏好评估和偏好实例化，以此框架统一分类现有对齐方法，识别设计权衡和失败模式。

Result: 构建了一个统一的对齐方法分类学，识别了跨先前对齐方法中观察到的重复设计权衡和失败模式，提炼了高层次设计原则，明确了管道设计选择如何影响优化信号。

Conclusion: 本文提出了对齐数据管道的分析框架，并指出了未来研究挑战，包括提示级对齐、智能体设置和演进目标下的对齐问题。

Abstract: Much of the alignment tuning literature is organized around optimization objectives, while the construction of alignment data is often treated implicitly. In this survey, we adopt a data centric perspective and reframe alignment tuning as a pipeline design problem. We decompose alignment data construction into three interacting stages, response synthesis, preference evaluation, and preference instantiation, and use this framework to organize existing alignment methods into a unified taxonomy. Through this lens, we identify recurring design trade-offs and failure modes observed across prior alignment methods, and distill a set of high level principles that clarify how pipeline design choices influence the resulting optimization signal. Finally, we outline open challenges for alignment data pipelines, including prompt-level alignment, agentic settings, and alignment under evolving objectives.

</details>


### [71] [MicroSpec: Accelerating Speculative Decoding with Lightweight In-Context Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26444)
*Zhiyang Chen,Daliang Xu,Yinyuan Zhang,Chenghua Wang,Mengwei Xu,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MicroSpec是一种无需训练的技术，通过为每个解码步骤构建紧凑的上下文敏感活动词汇表，大幅减少推测解码中的词汇表大小（平均减少40倍至3k个token），从而实现51.6%的草稿推理延迟减少和1.12-1.32倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常使用超过100k个token的词汇表，这在执行推测解码时的最终线性投影层造成了主要计算瓶颈。现有的词汇表剪枝方法依赖于固定或粗粒度的子词汇表，需要约30k个活动token来保持草稿模型质量。

Method: MicroSpec利用语言生成中自然的时间局部性，为每个解码步骤动态构建紧凑的上下文敏感活动词汇表。该方法无需额外训练参数，并通过共同设计的系统和算法减轻稀疏内存访问开销，使用异步收集和GPU驻留状态管理。

Result: MicroSpec将平均词汇表大小减少了40倍以上（降至3k个token以下），草稿推理延迟平均减少51.6%。在各项基准测试中，相对于领先的推测解码方法EAGLE-2实现了1.12-1.32倍的端到端加速，并且超越了更复杂的基于训练的剪枝基线。

Conclusion: MicroSpec作为一种即插即用的增强技术，通过上下文敏感的动态词汇表构建和硬件友好的系统设计，有效解决了推测解码中的计算瓶颈问题，在保持模型质量的同时显著提升了推理速度。

Abstract: Large language models typically employ vocabularies of over 100k tokens, which creates a major computational bottleneck at the final linear projection layer when performing speculative decoding. Current methods for vocabulary pruning depend on either fixed or coarse-grained sub-vocabularies, requiring around 30k active tokens to preserve the quality of the draft model. We introduce MicroSpec, a training-free technique that overcomes this limitation by building a compact, context-sensitive active vocabulary on the fly for every decoding step. Exploiting the natural temporal locality found in language generation, MicroSpec attains high token coverage while reducing the average vocabulary size by more than 40x (down to under 3k tokens), all without any additional trained parameters. To translate this high sparsity into actual speedups on contemporary hardware, we present a co-designed system and algorithm that mitigates the overhead of sparse memory accesses via asynchronous gathering and GPU-resident state management. Acting as a plug-and-play enhancement, MicroSpec reduces draft inference latency by 51.6% on average, achieving an end-to-end speedup of 1.12-1.32x relative to the leading speculative decoding approach EAGLE-2 on various benchmarks, while also surpassing more sophisticated training-based pruning baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Curation and Extraction of Drug-Related Entities from Reddit Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26445)
*Zewei Wang,Zihan Xu,Yishu Wei,Michael Chary,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReDose是一个包含6,435条Reddit药物使用帖子的数据集，用于提取药物、剂量和效果实体，旨在弥合临床知识和现实药物使用之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生主要从临床过量病例了解非法药物，这限制了对真实世界药物使用的理解。同时，药物使用者在网上分享第一手经验，提供了关于药物剂量和效果的宝贵见解。需要弥合临床知识和现实药物使用之间的差距。

Method: 构建了ReDose数据集，包含6,435条Reddit药物使用帖子。由一名委员会认证的毒理学家主要负责注释训练集和测试集，两名医学科学学生辅助测试集注释，标注DRUG、DOSE和EFFECT实体。使用BERT-based模型、大型语言模型(LLM)和检索增强生成(RAG)模型对6,267个注释进行了基准测试。

Result: BiomedBERT在DRUG实体提取上获得F1分数0.843。Llama-3 70B模型表现优于GPT-4(F1 = 0.79 vs. 0.72)。EFFECT提取仍然具有挑战性，GPT-4的召回率仅为0.41。

Conclusion: ReDose数据集通过捕获患者策划的叙述，有助于推进从社交媒体中提取医学数据的研究。该数据集弥合了临床知识和现实药物使用经验之间的信息差距，为药物信息提取提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: Physicians learn primarily about illicit drugs from clinical overdose cases, limiting their understanding of real-world usage. Meanwhile, drug users share first-hand experiences online, offering insights into dosage and effects of drugs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReDose (REddit Drug DOSe and Effect), a dataset of 6,435 Reddit posts on substance use.
