The April-May 2026 run evaluates Edge GulNawaz, Edge Latifa, OmniVoice clone, OmniVoice auto, and an Urdu negative control on 200 FLEURS and 200 filtered Common Voice 24 prompts. Under the independent omniASR_CTC_300M_v2, OmniVoice auto has the lowest WER (24.1% FLEURS, 27.4% CV24), followed by Edge GulNawaz (32.8%, 39.5%), Edge Latifa (35.6%, 47.7%), and OmniVoice clone (45.4%, 34.8%). WER below the natural-speech baseline reflects clean synthetic audio and should not be read as better than native speech. Whisper Large V3 returns 0.0% Pashto labels on checked Pashto TTS audio, while MMS-LID-4017 and SpeechBrain VoxLingua107 separate Pashto outputs from the Urdu control. The release provides provider metadata, per-sentence scores, LID audits, failure logs, and scripts for adding systems.

</details>


### [111] [Prompt Injection Detection is Regime-Dependent: A Deployment-Aware Evaluation with Interpretable Structural Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26999)
*Akindoyin Akinrele,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个部署感知的提示注入检测评估框架，比较了多种检测方法在多模型、多机制实验设置下的性能，发现检测效果高度依赖于部署机制和阈值选择，没有单一模型在所有设置中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入对大型语言模型的安全部署构成严重威胁，但现有检测方法通常在有限的设置下进行评估，不能反映实际部署环境中的约束条件。需要在更现实的部署条件下评估提示注入防御方法。

Method: 采用多模型、多机制实验框架：1) 比较词汇、语义、结构和基于Transformer的检测器；2) 在多个分布外设置、重复数据分割以及排序和阈值部署指标下评估；3) 引入可解释的结构信号来捕获层次覆盖、系统提示欺骗、角色重定义和逃避模式；4) 评估这些信号在稀疏模型和强编码器基线中的贡献。

Result: 检测性能高度依赖于部署机制，对阈值选择敏感，没有单一模型在所有设置中占优。基于Transformer的模型获得最强的整体性能，结构信号在某些机制中提供适度但一致的改进，在更困难的场景中改善低误报率行为。排序性能与部署有效性之间存在差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在现实操作约束下评估提示注入防御的重要性，突出了排序性能与部署有效性之间的差距。结构信号虽然效果有限但能提供稳定的增益，特别是在需要低误报率的场景中。

Abstract: Prompt injection poses a critical threat to the safe deployment of large language models, yet existing detection approaches are typically evaluated under limited settings that do not reflect real-world operating constraints. In this work, we present a deployment-aware evaluation of prompt injection detection using a multi-model and multi-regime experimental framework. We compare lexical, semantic, structural, and transformer-based detectors across multiple out-of-distribution settings, repeated data splits, and both ranking and thresholded deployment metrics. We introduce interpretable structural signals that capture hierarchy overrides, system prompt spoofing, role redefinition, and evasion patterns, and assess their contribution both within sparse models and in combination with strong encoder baselines. Our results show that detection performance is highly regime-dependent and sensitive to threshold selection, with no single model dominating across all settings. Transformer-based models achieve the strongest overall performance, while structural signals provide modest but consistent gains in certain regimes and improve low false positive rate behaviour in harder scenarios. These findings highlight the gap between ranking performance and deployment effectiveness and underscore the importance of evaluating prompt injection defences under realistic operational constraints. Code will be released.

</details>


### [112] [Cast a Wider Net: Coordinated Pass@K Policy Optimization for Code Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27000)
*Yilong Li,Suman Banerjee,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CPPO（Coordinated Pass@K Policy Optimization）通过协调的规划和求解策略，提高代码生成中pass@K的效率，减少重复尝试并提升问题解决成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码生成方法使用单个答案分布独立采样K次，这导致重复的推理路径，浪费计算资源，特别是在竞争性编程中许多问题存在多种算法策略，而pass@K只需要一次正确尝试。

Method: 提出CPPO框架，将pass@K生成转化为策略空间的联合探索：1）规划器生成K=4个替代性高级方法元组；2）共享求解器为每个方法尝试一次解决方案；3）使用乘法规划器奖励R_plan = J_ψ·R_out训练联合策略，仅对能导致验证器确认pass@K成功的有效策略元组分配信用。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests和LiveCodeBench-v6三个基准测试上，CPPO在相同K=4求解尝试预算下，相较于直接采样、规划基线、仅规划器SFT和pass@K导向的RL方法，显著提升了pass@4性能。在9个模型-基准测试单元中有6个获得统计学显著增益，最大单次增益为Qwen3.5-9B在LiveCodeBench-v6上比最强基线PKPO提升+0.16（0.588→0.748，p<0.05）。

Conclusion: CPPO通过协调策略空间探索，有效解决了传统pass@K方法中冗余采样的问题，在代码生成任务中实现了更高效的测试时计算分配，显著提升了问题解决的成功率。

Abstract: Repeated sampling with a verifier is the standard way to allocate test-time compute for code generation, with pass@$K$ as the canonical metric. Yet the standard policy class draws $K$ independent samples from a single answer distribution, so attempts often collapse onto near-duplicate reasoning paths and waste the budget on redundant rollouts. This failure is costly in competitive programming, where many problems admit multiple distinct algorithmic strategies and pass@$K$ requires only one correct attempt. We propose Coordinated Pass@$K$ Policy Optimization (CPPO), which turns pass@$K$ generation into joint exploration over strategies: a planner emits a tuple of $K{=}4$ alternative high-level methods, and a shared solver attempts one solution per method. CPPO trains this joint policy with a multiplicative planner reward, $R_{\mathrm{plan}} = J_ψ\cdot R_{\mathrm{out}}$, assigning credit only to valid strategy tuples that lead to verifier-confirmed pass@$K$ success. Across APPS, CodeContests, and LiveCodeBench-v6, CPPO improves pass@$4$ over direct sampling, planning baselines, planner-only SFT, and pass@$K$-oriented RL under the same $K{=}4$ solver-attempt budget, with statistically significant gains on six of nine model--benchmark cells. The largest single gain is $+0.16$ on Qwen3.5-9B LiveCodeBench-v6 over the strongest baseline, PKPO ($0.588 \rightarrow 0.748$; paired bootstrap, $p < 0.05$).

</details>


### [113] [PersLitEval: Fine-grained Benchmark and Evaluation of LLMs on Persian Literature Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27015)
*Ruhallah Niazi,Faeze Ghorbanpour,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了PersLitEval波斯文学评估基准，包含4,514道多项选择题，涵盖八个细粒度类别，用于评估LLM在波斯文学知识上的表现，发现模型在概念理解任务上表现较好，但在形式语言分析（特别是拼写和构词）上表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型具有令人印象深刻的多语言能力，但在非英语语言的文学知识评估方面仍然表现不足。特别是波斯文学知识的评估缺失，现有基准无法充分评估LLM在这方面的能力。

Method: 引入PersLitEval基准，包含4,514道波斯文学多项选择题，涵盖拼写、文学手法、语法、词汇、构词和概念理解等八个细粒度类别，数据来源于Konkur大学入学考试材料。评估了六个LLM在十种提示策略下的表现。

Result: 模型在不同任务难度层级上存在显著类别差异：在概念相似性任务上准确率较高，但在形式语言分析（特别是拼写和构词）上表现最差。提示策略对性能有显著影响，带有解释的少样本示例效果最好，尤其是在形式语言类别上。错误分析识别出三种失败模式：语义理解差距、形式语言知识差距和计数/枚举错误。

Conclusion: LLM在非英语文学知识评估方面存在显著能力差距，不同任务类别需要不同的改进策略。PersLitEval基准为评估和改进LLM在波斯文学等非英语语言能力提供了重要工具，强调了针对特定语言特征进行模型优化的重要性。

Abstract: Despite impressive multilingual capabilities, large language models (LLMs) remain poorly evaluated on literary knowledge in non-English languages. We introduce PersLitEval, a benchmark of 4,514 Persian literature multiple-choice questions across eight fine-grained categories spanning spelling, literary devices, grammar, vocabulary, word formation, and conceptual understanding, sourced from materials for the Konkur university entrance examination. We evaluate six LLMs across ten prompting strategies, revealing striking category-level disparities across three tiers of task difficulty: models reach higher accuracy on conceptual similarity tasks but struggle with formal linguistic analysis, with spelling and word formation proving the hardest across all models. Prompting strategy has a significant impact on performance, with explained few-shot examples yielding the best results, particularly on formal linguistic categories. An error analysis identifies three failure modes: semantic comprehension gaps, formal linguistic knowledge gaps, and counting/enumeration errors, suggesting that different categories require different improvement strategies.

</details>


### [114] [Attribute-Based Diagnosis of LLM Alignment with Hate Speech Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27025)
*Mohammad Amine Jradi,Faeze Ghorbanpour,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在仇恨言论标注中表现出模式化偏差：行为维度与人类标注强相关，而评价维度呈系统性反转。通过属性分解和置信度加权回归，能比端到端标注获得更好的人类对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论的人工标注成本高、主观性强且标注者间一致性差，导致大规模数据集构建困难。需要系统评估LLM在主观属性标注上是否与人类判断对齐，并探索有效的LLM标注方法。

Method: 1) 系统分析LLaMA 3.1和Qwen 2.5的大小变体在10个理论基础的主观属性上与人类判断的对齐程度；2) 测试人口统计人物条件调节对模型置信度和对齐性的影响；3) 提出通过置信度加权Ridge回归结合属性级LLM预测来重建连续仇恨分数。

Result: 发现所有模型均呈现一致分化：行为维度（侮辱、羞辱、攻击-防御）与人类标注强相关，而评价维度（尊重、情感、仇恨言论）则系统性反转。人口统计人物条件调节降低了模型置信度但未改善对齐。提出的方法在Measuring Hate Speech语料库上达到R²=0.71，优于直接提示基线。

Conclusion: LLM在仇恨言论标注中存在结构性偏差，但通过有结构地分解主观属性并采用统计方法结合多属性预测，能够获得比端到端标注更丰富、更人类对齐的信号。属性分解策略为解决LLM在主观任务中的系统性偏差提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Hate speech annotation is costly, subjective, and prone to annotator disagreement, making large-scale dataset construction challenging. We systematically analyze how well large language models (LLMs) align with human judgments across ten theoretically grounded subjective attributes, such as dehumanization, violence, and sentiment, evaluating both small and large variants of Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5. Our analysis reveals a consistent split across all models: behaviorally explicit dimensions (insult, humiliate, attack-defend) correlate strongly with human annotations, while evaluative dimensions (respect, sentiment, hate speech) are systematically inverted. Demographic persona conditioning reduces model confidence without improving alignment. Building on these insights, we propose combining attribute-level LLM predictions via a confidence-weighted Ridge regression to reconstruct continuous hate speech scores from the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, achieving $R^2$ of up to 0.71 and outperforming direct prompting baselines, demonstrating that structured attribute decomposition recovers a richer and more human-aligned signal than end-to-end label prediction alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrickAnything是一个几何条件自回归框架，从多样的3D表示生成可建造的砖块结构，通过点云作为统一几何接口，采用结构感知树状标记化和约束解码提高可建造性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的砖块生成方法存在局限：启发式优化在目标3D形状不满足约束时会失效，而现有序列生成方法没有显式建模底层3D几何和装配关系。需要一种能处理几何重建、离散部件约束和结构稳定性统一要求的解决方案。

Method: 提出BrickAnything框架，使用点云作为统一几何接口，预测满足装配约束的砖块序列。引入结构感知树状标记化，通过局部附着关系表示砖块结构，使序列生成更符合物理建造过程。采用偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚来改善稳定性和几何保真度。

Result: 实验表明BrickAnything能产生几何保真度高且物理可实现的砖块结构，所提出的标记化相比传统排序策略有效减少了回滚和重新生成次数。

Conclusion: BrickAnything通过几何条件自回归框架和结构感知标记化，解决了从3D形状生成可建造砖块结构的挑战，平衡了几何重建、部件约束和结构稳定性要求，为物理建造提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [116] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文质疑大语言模型是否真正具备内省能力，认为现有研究混淆了真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配。通过重新审视两个评估范式，作者发现当前证据不足以证明LLM具备元认知监控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多研究声称大语言模型能够检测和报告自己的内部状态，但作者从人类元认知研究中吸取教训，认为这一结论可能为时过早。需要区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，而且仅靠行为证据本身不足以确立强内省主张。

Method: 重新审视两种近期引入的评估范式：1) 模型检测内部状态是否被篡改，发现模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操纵；2) 模型预测基于自身隐藏状态的标签，发现仅访问输入的分类器能达到与模型自身情境预测相同的性能。进一步引入重新标记的控制设置，模型必须依赖内部表征而非任务语义来解决问题。

Result: 1) 模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操纵，表明原研究的成功反映的是检测异常的一般能力，而非特定检测内部状态干预的能力；2) 仅使用输入的分类器性能与模型自身预测相当，表明原结果不能确证模型对其内部表征具有特权访问权；3) 在重新标记的控制设置中，模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以确立大语言模型显示元认知监控能力。需要更好的实验设计来区分真正内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，仅行为证据无法证明强内省主张。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [117] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM基准测试直接评估LLMs中构建他人信念表征的能力，通过要求明确建模叙事中所有相关角色的信念结构，分为信念提取和信念标注两个阶段，揭示当前LLMs在将叙事事实转化为角色信念方面存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论心智（ToM）评估主要采用端点问答范式，仅根据最终答案判断性能，这种范式无法确定模型是否真正构建了底层心理状态表征，特别是在涉及分歧、演化或错误信念的场景中。需要直接评估这些表征以理解LLMs的ToM能力。

Method: 引入OmniToM基准测试：1）包含信念命题的最小陈述格式；2）两阶段评估：信念提取（从故事中提取相关信念）和信念标注（为每个信念分配七维模式标签：递归顺序、真值状态、知识访问、明确性、内容类型、心理来源、上下文）；3）基于895个ToMBench故事和22,343个标注信念命题；4）使用人类校准的LLM辅助标注流程。

Result: 在零样本评估中，OmniToM揭示了角色特定信念追踪瓶颈：当前LLMs在将叙事事实转化为角色信念和共享心理状态所需的知识访问和表征决策方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 端点问答范式无法充分评估LLMs的理论心智能力，OmniToM通过直接评估信念表征提供了一个更精确的评估框架，揭示了当前模型在信念建模方面的具体局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [118] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个原型框架，用于管理LLM生成的虚拟实验室程序知识中的不确定性，通过结构化领域表示和不确定状态转换样本来提取候选程序规则，转化为可检查的约束，并修复不确定的程序步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育虚拟实验室可以使实验培训更具扩展性、适应性和可访问性，但创作新的模拟实验室程序成本高昂。大型语言模型可以协助生成详细的实验程序，但它们的输出不应被视为可直接执行的计划，因为这些输出可能遗漏必要步骤、步骤顺序错误或产生与实验室设备不兼容的逻辑错误指令。

Method: 提出一个原型框架，使用结构化领域表示和不确定的LLM生成的状态转换样本来提取候选程序规则，将这些规则转化为明确且可检查的约束，并利用它们修复不确定的程序步骤。

Result: 该框架在虚拟实验室领域中得到演示，涉及实验室仪器、容器、工具和材料转移操作。尽管动机领域是教育虚拟实验室，但基础问题更普遍：管理结构化交互环境中用于行动规划的不确定程序知识。

Conclusion: 该框架为处理LLM生成的程序知识中的不确定性提供了一种系统性方法，有助于提高虚拟实验室程序的可执行性和可靠性，解决了在结构化交互环境中管理不确定程序知识这一更普遍的问题。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [119] [ScientistOne: Towards Human-Level Autonomous Research via Chain-of-Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26340)
*Rui Meng,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Chun-Liang Li,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Rajarishi Sinha,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Burak Gokturk,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScientistOne是一个端到端的自主研究系统，通过证据链框架确保研究输出的可验证性，包括零幻觉引用、完美的分数验证和高方法-代码一致性，在多个前沿研究任务中达到或超过人类专家性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统存在表面评估难以检测的可验证性失败：包括伪造引用、不可复现的分数以及方法与实现不一致。这些系统性缺陷使得研究成果难以验证和信任。

Method: 提出了三个关键贡献：1) 证据链框架，要求每个主张都能追溯到其证据来源；2) ScientistOne系统，在文献综述、方案发现和论文写作过程中构建性地维护证据链；3) CoE审计，包含分数验证、规范违反、引用验证和方法-代码对齐四项完整性检查。

Result: 在涵盖5个系统和5个前沿研究任务的75篇论文评估中：基线系统至少存在一种系统性失败模式（幻觉引用率高达21%，分数验证通过率最低42%，方法-代码对齐率20%-80%）。而ScientistOne实现零幻觉引用(0/337)、完美分数验证(12/12)和最高方法-代码对齐(14/15)，在所有5个任务中达到或超过人类专家性能。在医学影像等6个额外任务上也取得SOTA表现。

Conclusion: 证据链框架是解决自主研究系统可验证性问题的有效方法。ScientistOne通过构建性维护证据链实现了显著的可靠性和可验证性改进，为可信自主研究系统的发展提供了重要范例。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents produce competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts, yet their outputs contain verifiability failures undetectable by surface-level evaluation: fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, and method descriptions that diverge from the implementation. We address this through three contributions. First, Chain-of-Evidence (CoE), a verifiability framework requiring every claim to be traceable to its evidence source. Second, ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system that maintains evidence chains by construction throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing. Third, CoE Audit, a post-hoc audit whose four integrity checks -- score verification, specification violation, reference verification, and method-code alignment -- apply uniformly to all systems. Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier research tasks, every baseline exhibits at least one systematic failure mode: hallucinated reference rates reach 21%, score verification passes in as few as 42% of papers, and method-code alignment ranges from 20% to 80%. ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and the highest method-code alignment (14/15), while matching or exceeding human expert performance on all five tasks. ScientistOne further generalizes to six additional tasks spanning medical imaging, fine-grained recognition, 3D perception, and language modeling, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench tasks where baselines fail entirely.

</details>


### [120] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动选择中间层的FEPoID准则，用于提升大语言模型幻觉检测性能，并引入截断策略进一步放大幻觉相关信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，幻觉相关信号在LLM中间层比最终层更强编码，但如何自动选择高性能层仍缺乏系统方法。当前基于直觉的层选择标准在不同架构、规模和任务上表现不一致，需要开发更加可靠的选择准则。

Method: 1) 提出并评估了几种基于直觉假设的层选择标准；2) 设计了新的FEPoID（内蕴维度第一有效峰值）准则，该方法无需训练且计算开销极小；3) 研究了LLM生成行为，提出简单的截断策略以增强幻觉信号。

Result: 提出的FEPoID准则能一致性地识别出最优或接近最优的中间层，优于其他直觉标准及现有幻觉检测基线方法。结合截断策略，显著提升了问答和摘要任务上的幻觉检测性能。

Conclusion: FEPoID提供了一种高效、可靠且无需训练的层选择方法，解决了LLM中间层自动选择的难题，配合截断策略进一步优化了幻觉检测性能，为相关研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [121] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARL算法通过利用局部动态直觉学习可重用技能，在复杂人形环境中实现有意义的技能聚类，并在OGBench基准测试中提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层强化学习通过发现和重用时间扩展技能来解决长期强化学习任务，但获取真正可重用的技能仍是一个开放挑战。需要利用局部动态直觉的抽象：不同全局上下文中的局部转换需要类似的动作序列。

Method: 提出CARL算法（基于对比动作的可重用局部控制表示），通过对齐需要相似动作序列的上下文，学习哪些技能可以重用以及在何处重用。该算法可与多种分层强化学习算法结合，其中高层策略需要对底层技能进行推理。

Result: 在复杂人形环境中实现有意义的技能定性聚类，当与HIQL结合时，在OGBench基准测试上展示出改进的下游性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部动态直觉和对齐相似上下文，CARL能够有效学习可重用技能表示，为分层强化学习中技能重用问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [122] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MM-CreativityBench基准测试，用于评估多模态模型在视觉丰富、物理约束环境中的创意工具使用能力。研究发现当前LMMs在持续接地探索方面存在不足，并提出了基于可供性接地的对齐方法来改善性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型（LMMs）在感知和推理方面进展迅速，但其在开放环境中的创意问题解决能力仍不清楚。智能不仅需要回答明确问题，更需要识别场景元素如何在非显然但物理可行的方式下重新利用。这种创意问题解决是人类智能的核心，但在当前基准测试中缺乏评估。

Method: 1. 提出MM-CreativityBench基准测试，用于评估可供性接地的创意工具使用能力，包含场景图像和结构化视图
2. 实验发现当前LMMs的失败模式：缺乏持续接地探索、忽视相关实体、检查不足或产生幻觉
3. 提出可供性接地的对齐方法：将创意工具使用作为偏好学习问题，使用直接偏好优化（DPO）鼓励基于视觉证据的推理
4. 整合从可供性知识库衍生的监督来引导更广泛的实体探索和多轮规划

Result: 实验结果显示，与基线模型相比，所提方法在选择正确实体和部件方面取得一致提升，同时显著减少幻觉和接地相关错误。模型能够更好地基于视觉证据进行属性-可供性推理，而不是产生幻觉替代方案。

Conclusion: 论文强调了评估LMMs创意问题解决能力的重要性，揭示了当前模型在持续接地探索方面的局限性。提出的可供性接地对齐方法有效提升了模型在视觉丰富、物理约束环境中的创意工具使用性能，为多模态模型的接地推理能力发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [123] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发了MedGuideX模型，通过将临床实践指南转化为可执行的决策逻辑来生成事实和反事实训练数据，从而提高医学大语言模型的临床推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将临床实践指南作为自由文本训练数据或检索源使用，未能充分利用其程序化决策结构。为了更好地利用这种结构，需要开发能够有效提取并应用指南中决策逻辑的方法

Method: 提出指南衍生训练管道：1)将临床实践指南转化为可执行的临床决策逻辑；2)利用该逻辑生成事实和反事实的问答数据；3)在医学LLM上进行后续训练，生成MedGuideX模型

Result: 1) 在四个临床推理基准测试中，MedGuideX实现了10.28%的相对准确率提升；2) 医生评估显示MedGuideX更好地恢复了临床医生编写的推理步骤，并在忠实性、有效性、完整性和清晰度方面产生医生偏爱的推理

Conclusion: 从临床实践指南中提取的可执行决策逻辑可以转化为可扩展的监督信号，用于构建可靠的医学大语言模型，这种结构化方法显著提升了模型的临床推理能力

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [124] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Tail-Aware HiFloat4：基于ViDiT-Q量化流程适配Wan2.2模型，采用W4A4 HiFloat4格式，通过尾部感知百分比校准和PTQ状态恢复减少校准异常值影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低比特文本到视频生成量化挑战，需要在高性能视频生成模型中实现高效的4位量化，同时保持模型质量

Method: 1. 将ViDiT-Q后训练量化流程适配到Wan2.2模型；2. 对Wan2.2 transformer主线性层采用W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化；3. 保持数值敏感边界模块为高精度；4. 引入激活尾部感知百分比校准模块进行通道掩码构建；5. 结合紧凑PTQ状态恢复

Result: 设计减少了罕见校准异常值的影响，同时保持运行时HiFloat4算术和采样流程不变

Conclusion: Tail-Aware HiFloat4为低比特文本到视频生成提供了一种有效的量化方案，通过尾部感知校准和PTQ状态恢复实现了质量与效率的平衡

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [125] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UnityMAS-O：一个通用的强化学习优化框架，用于LLM多智能体系统，将完整工作流作为优化单元而非单个响应，支持用户定义多智能体工作流的结构化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多智能体系统主要通过提示、工具和控制规则手动协调，缺乏统一的强化学习接口进行优化。现有RL后训练框架主要针对单策略优化，缺乏对用户定义多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色特定信用分配和可配置参数共享的抽象。

Method: 1. 将完整工作流作为优化单元而非单个响应或策略轨迹；2. 通过四个一等对象表示工作流：逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励、智能体-模型映射；3. 逻辑智能体与物理模型参数解耦，支持完全共享、完全分离和部分共享；4. 基于Ray的星形拓扑运行时架构，中央控制器执行工作流、调用工具、记录结构化轨迹、组装奖励，模型本地工作机组处理滚存、缓冲、优势计算和分布式PPO风格更新。

Result: 在检索增强QA、迭代智能体搜索和反思性代码生成任务上实例化UnityMAS-O。在Natural Questions、HotpotQA和代码任务上，多智能体RL在优化后显著改善了手动指定的工作流，对于较小模型和严格的代码全通过指标获得了特别大的提升。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可以作为可复用的底层系统，将多样化的基于LLM的多智能体工作流转换为可训练的多智能体RL系统，证明了其在优化多智能体系统方面的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [126] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了具有累积损伤的长视界决策问题，识别了策略梯度方法在此类问题中的两种正交失败模式，提出了将完成度（到达终端视界）与最优性（给定完成度下的动态规划参考）分离的分解方法，并在两个不同领域的环境中进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界决策问题中，局部吸引的行动与全局不利的结果相耦合。策略梯度方法在此类问题上存在系统性失败模式，需要深入理解这些失败的原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出分解方法将问题分为完成度（是否到达终端视界）和最优性（给定完成度下的策略质量）。使用带有线性软惩罚的PPO算法，通过控制视界访问和动作空间限制进行实验验证。在两个不同领域的环境（49步砖匠职业和20赛季NBA大前锋职业）中测试四个可验证的预测。

Result: 1. 仅授予视界访问会降低完成率；2. 动作空间限制结合视界访问可实现完成但留下最优性差距（ΔM_final = 0.271），源于损伤原点的第一阶段贪婪承诺；3. 四个预测在两种环境中均定性复制；4. 视界不变性预测在四个测试视界中的三个成立，H=15的例外与H*边界一致（NBA参数下H*∈[6,14]）。

Conclusion: 通过将完成度与最优性分离的分解方法，论文揭示了策略梯度方法在累积损伤问题中的两种正交失败模式。研究结果提供了对这类决策问题系统性失败的深入理解，并为改进算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [127] [A Dataset of Robot-Patient and Doctor-Patient Medical Dialogues for Spoken Language Processing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26747)
*Heriberto Cuayahuitl,Grace Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MeDial-Speech数据集，这是一个包含111+小时真实医疗对话语音的数据集，用于训练和评估医疗AI系统，并设计了基于句子选择的对话基准来测试GPT-5 mini、DeepSeek-V3和Claude Sonnet 4等LLMs的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用任务上表现出色，但在文本或语音医疗咨询中的应用仍是一个开放的研究问题。缺乏专门用于医疗对话评估的现实语音数据集和评估基准，限制了医疗AI系统的开发和研究。

Method: 1) 创建MeDial-Speech数据集：从机器人-患者和医生-患者对话中收集真实环境下的语音数据，涵盖四种健康状况（路易体痴呆、心力衰竭、肩痛、心绞痛），共计111+小时；2) 设计对话基准：通过20选项的句子选择任务来评估模型；3) 评估三个SOTA LLMs（GPT-5 mini、DeepSeek-V3、Claude Sonnet 4）的性能。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4在句子选择任务中表现最佳，人工转录准确率为71.1%，自动转录准确率为74.7%。所有LLMs在概率预测中都表现出高度过度自信，无论选择正确还是错误的医疗对话句子。数据集在HuggingFace上免费提供非商业用途使用。

Conclusion: MeDial-Speech为医疗AI的对话系统研究提供了有价值的资源，实验结果表明当前LLMs在医疗对话理解方面仍有改进空间，特别是在校准其置信度方面。该数据集有望推动更可靠、可评估的医疗对话AI系统发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have brought huge improvements to Artificial Intelligence (AI), which can be applied to general-purpose tasks. However, their application to textual or spoken medical consultations is still an open research problem. This paper proposes MeDial-Speech, a novel speech dataset for training and evaluating Med-AIs that can carry out consultations with patients. It was collected in realistic environments from robot-patient and doctor-patient dialogues, contains 111+ hours of speech data (without data augmentation), and covers four health conditions: Lewy body dementia, heart failure, shoulder pain, and angina. In addition, we propose a dialogue benchmark via sentence selection (with 20 options) to evaluate three state-of-the-art LLMs: GPT-5 mini, DeepSeek-V3, and Claude Sonnet 4. Experimental results reveal that Claude Sonnet 4 is the best in sentence selection, with 71.1% accuracy using manual transcriptions and 74.7% using automatic transcriptions, and that all LLMs are highly overconfident in their probabilistic predictions, regardless of selecting correct or incorrect sentences in medical dialogues. This dataset is free of charge for non-commercial purposes at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/hcuayahu/MeDial-Speech

</details>


### [128] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型(LRMs)中的拒绝行为不仅依赖于激活空间的定向子空间，还受到链式思维(CoT)的主动强化，形成联合编码机制，使模型对单纯激活干预具有更强鲁棒性，但暴露出通过CoT进行表面攻击的潜在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过生成链式思维(CoT)轨迹再产生最终输出，这一动态内部状态机制使传统拒绝控制机制复杂化。研究发现，与指令调优LLMs中拒绝仅由单一方向子空间介导不同，LRMs中的拒绝行为额外依赖于CoT，这一特性需要深入理解其机制和潜在安全影响。

Method: 研究采用激活导向干预方法，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B模型上进行实验：1) 固定CoT时激活导向干预；2) 完全移除CoT后干预；3) 两阶段干预(模型在激活导向下重新生成CoT)。通过量化拒绝反转率分析CoT在拒绝机制中的作用。

Result: 实验结果：1) 固定CoT时激活导向仅能反转39%的拒绝；2) 移除CoT后拒绝反转率提升至70%；3) 两阶段干预(重新生成CoT)下拒绝反转率达94%；4) 重新生成的CoT即使在激活导向移除后仍保留48%的干预效果。这表明CoT能独立承载和重建合规信号。

Conclusion: LRMs中的拒绝机制在残差流激活和CoT中形成联合编码。这种联合编码使模型对单纯激活层面的干预具有更强鲁棒性，但也暴露了通过CoT进行替代表面攻击的潜在漏洞，为LRM安全机制设计和对抗防御提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>


### [129] [The Attribution Blind Spot: Detecting When Language Models Rely on Memory Rather Than Retrieved Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26778)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Bo Yang,Chen Ye,Gaolei Li,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出计算现实监控(CRM)方法，通过比较模型在有/无检索上下文时的内部表征差异来检测归因盲点，解决检索增强生成中无法验证上下文是否真正影响生成的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)需要验证检索到的上下文是否真正影响了模型的生成，但当前方法存在归因盲点：当检索文档与模型预训练数据重叠时，模型可能仅从参数记忆中生成看似一致的输出，导致无法区分生成是否真正基于检索上下文。

Method: 提出计算现实监控(CRM)方法，借鉴认知科学中的现实监控框架，通过比较语言模型在有检索上下文和无检索上下文时的内部表征差异，检测是否存在基于预训练暴露的轨迹签名。该方法不认证个体生成使用的来源，而是检测预训练暴露是否留下可测量的内部轨迹信号。

Result: 在三个模型家族的九个变体上进行测试，发现表征差异集中在架构特定的层模式中，通过块级噪声干预获得收敛支持，能够跨任务和数据集泛化，但在领域混淆的基准测试中失效。归因盲点是可测量的且部分可解决。

Conclusion: 内部表征携带了输出层面无法看到的诊断信号，这为建立基于证据来源内部认知来指导外部行为的系统奠定了基础，是解决归因盲点的关键进展。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation promises to ground language model outputs in external evidence, yet the field has no reliable way to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation -- a prerequisite for any high-stakes deployment. The standard assumption, that context-consistent output implies context-governed output, breaks when the retrieved document overlaps with the model's pretraining data: the model can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory, and both pathways yield indistinguishable output. We name this failure the attribution blind spot and introduce Computational Reality Monitoring (CRM) to address it. CRM operationalizes a principle adapted from cognitive science's reality monitoring framework: comparing internal representations with and without context reveals membership-conditioned representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss. CRM does not certify which source an individual generation used; it detects whether pretraining exposure leaves a measurable internal trajectory signature, establishing a necessary substrate for source attribution. Across nine model variants spanning three families, this divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns, receives converging support from block-level noise intervention, and generalizes across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks. The attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable: internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, establishing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior.

</details>


### [130] [LiveK12Bench: Have Large Multimodal Models Truly Conquered High School-level Examinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26781)
*Xiaohan Wang,Mingze Yin,Yilin Zhao,Gang Liu,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveK12Bench是一个动态、全面的跨学科基准测试，用于评估大型多模态模型在真实考试场景中的推理能力，包含2000+个来自最新真实试卷的问题，并提出了创新的"模拟考试"评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试无法捕捉真实考试环境的复杂性：大多是静态的、容易受到数据污染、局限于受限的模态、学科和评估标准。需要评估大型多模态模型作为智能导师在现实世界考试中的表现。

Method: 1) 开发自动化管道持续摄取和解析最新考试试卷以减轻数据泄露；2) 提出新颖的"模拟考试"评估方案，评估模型在端到端考试中完成准确高效推理路径的能力；3) 包含数学、物理、化学和生物的2000+个经过验证的问题。

Result: 对12个大型多模态模型的广泛实验表明，高级模型在考试现实约束下表现显著下降：GPT-5的得分从79下降到53（满分100），当同时评估过程严谨性和效率时。模型对复杂视觉布局的敏感性等关键脆弱性被暴露。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了理想推理能力与真实教育准备度之间的差距，高级模型在现实考试约束下存在重大性能退化。LiveK12Bench强调了评估模型在实际教育场景中表现的重要性，代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Advanced Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in K-12 reasoning tasks, exhibiting great promise as intelligent tutors. Realizing this potential requires models to navigate real-world examinations effectively, yet most existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of authentic testing environments. Specifically, most datasets are static, prone to data contamination, and are often confined to restricted modalities, disciplines, and evaluation criteria. To address these issues, we introduce LiveK12Bench, a dynamic, holistic, multi-disciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning abilities of LMMs in realistic examination scenarios. LiveK12Bench comprises 2K+ verified questions spanning Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, sourced from the latest real-world exam papers and designed to grow over time. Our framework features several core innovations: 1) featuring an automated pipeline that continuously ingests and parses the latest examination papers to mitigate data leakage; and 2) proposing a novel `Mock Exam' evaluation scheme, which assesses the ability to complete end-to-end exams autonomously with accurate and efficient reasoning paths. Extensive experiments on 12 LMMs reveal that advanced models suffer substantial performance degradation under exam-realistic constraints: GPT-5's score drops from 79 to 53 (out of 100) when process rigor and efficiency are jointly evaluated. Our findings expose critical vulnerabilities, such as sensitivity to complex visual layouts, highlighting the gap between idealized reasoning capabilities and true educational readiness. Both code and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [131] [Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Jie Chen,Hongzhi Wang,Xuyang Teng,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有后训练评估方法的局限性，通过引入双重门控协议来分解模型的多跳推理能力，发现组合崩溃现象在聚合指标中不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练评估主要依赖聚合基准分数，将多跳推理视为单一能力进行测量。这种假设忽略了模型在组合已知事实时可能出现的系统性失败，即"组合崩溃"现象，而这些在聚合指标中是不可见的。

Method: 提出双重门控协议，将评估对象从聚合组合性差距转变为基于稳定原子访问的残差组合失败。该方法将后训练增益分解为三个独立通道：原子稳定性、残差组合和临界深度。在包含深度2-11的时间事实链基准上，对四种后训练方案进行测试，并结合诊断性探测分析。

Result: 研究发现具有统计不可区分原子知识的训练方案在组合行为上存在超过40个百分点的差异，揭示了组合崩溃现象。分解分析表明后训练目标会改变组合能力方向，而这些变化被聚合指标掩盖。诊断性探测显示相当部分的组合失败反映了生成时计算约束而非永久性的组合能力缺失。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数不能准确评估多跳推理能力，需要采用原子门控的组合度量来更细致地评估后训练效果。关于多跳推理改进的声明应伴随这种分解分析，以确保评估的准确性。

Abstract: Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into chains, invisible to aggregate metrics. We introduce a double-gate protocol that changes the estimand from an aggregate compositionality gap to residual composition failure conditioned on stable atomic access, decomposing post-training gains into three independent channels: atomic stability, residual composition, and critical depth. On a benchmark of temporal factual chains spanning depths 2--11 across four post-training recipes, this decomposition reveals that post-training objectives shift composition capability in directions that aggregate metrics mask, and suggests that claims about multi-hop reasoning improvement should be accompanied by atomic-gate-controlled composition metrics. Diagnostic probes further show that a substantial share of measured composition failure reflects generation-time computation constraints rather than permanent inability to compose.

</details>


### [132] [What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795)
*Xiang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现：思维链提示中性能提升主要源于词汇激活和短程令牌共现效应，而非句子层面的逻辑推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链提示能可靠提升语言模型准确性，但驱动这种改善的理性文本特性尚不清楚。先前研究主要关注生成时行为，本文转而研究推理时行为：给定固定的理性文本上下文，其中的什么改变了答案？

Method: 采用推理时探针方法，通过对比实验分析理性文本的不同特性对答案的影响。包括：全局词汇洗牌实验、结构化文本分析（保留不同长度连续令牌窗口）、排除显式答案声明复制效应、排除完整语法实现影响等。

Result: 1. 即使全局词汇洗牌的理性文本也显著优于无理性文本基线，表明强烈的词汇激活效应。2. 结构化文本的额外增益主要来自短程令牌邻接而非句子级逻辑排序：仅保留2-3个令牌的连续窗口即可恢复大部分思维链性能增益。3. 排除了显式答案声明复制、答案值复制或完整语法实现作为主要驱动因素。4. 该定性模式在多个模型家族、参数规模和数据集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 思维链在推理时的观察收益主要源于词汇激活和短程令牌共现，而非句子级逻辑推导。这支持局部共现激活解释模型，挑战了思维链主要依靠推理过程提升性能的传统理解。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong lexical activation effect. More importantly, the additional gain from structured text appears to arise less from sentence-level logical ordering and more from short-range token adjacency. Preserving contiguous windows of just $n^\star{=}2$--$3$ tokens recovers most of the remaining gain toward full CoT performance. Supporting experiments rule out copying of explicit answer declarations or answer values, as well as full grammatical realization, as primary drivers. Further generalization experiments show that the qualitative pattern remains stable across multiple model families, parameter scales, and datasets. These results support a local co-occurrence activation (LCA) account of probe-time CoT, in which the observed gains appear to arise primarily from lexical activation and short-range token co-occurrence rather than sentence-level logical derivation.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Stakeholder LLM Alignment: Decomposing Estimation from Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26878)
*Lulu Zheng,Wenjin Yang,Xiangwen Zhang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Zheng Pan,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DecompR方法，通过解耦多利益相关者评估中的效用估计和效用聚合过程，使用反事实校准权重来消除候选相关权重漂移，从而提高评估稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多利益相关者任务中，单一输出需要满足具有冲突偏好的用户。现有的整体LLM评估方法将效用估计和效用聚合混为一谈，产生了不稳定的隐式权重。当利益相关者满意度分散时，这种特定于聚合的"权重噪声"会导致较大的分数偏移，且偏移随利益相关者数量增加而加剧。

Method: 提出了DecompR方法：1) 基于查询结构在候选评分前固定反事实校准权重；2) 独立估计每个角色的效用。该方法消除了候选相关的权重漂移，减少了估计噪声。

Result: 通过理论和实证分析表明，权重噪声在利益相关者满意度分散时会产生较大的分数偏移，且偏移随利益相关者数量增加。DecompR方法通过分离权重确定和效用估计过程，有效减少了权重诱导的分数偏移和估计噪声。

Conclusion: 多利益相关者LLM评估需要解耦效用估计和聚合过程。DecompR通过预先固定基于查询结构的反事实校准权重，然后独立估计各角色效用的方法，显著提高了评估的稳定性和可靠性，减少了权重噪声带来的影响。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder tasks require one output to satisfy users with conflicting preferences. Holistic LLM judges conflate utility estimation and utility aggregation, yielding unstable implicit weights. We show empirically and theoretically that this aggregation-specific \emph{weighting noise} can create large score shifts when stakeholder satisfaction is dispersed; in our experiments, these weight-induced shifts also increase with stakeholder count. We propose \textsc{DecompR}: counterfactual-calibrated weights are fixed from query structure before candidate scoring, while per-role utilities are estimated independently, removing candidate-dependent weight drift and reducing estimation noise.

</details>


### [134] [On the Detection of Commutative Factors in Factor Graphs: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26908)
*Malte Luttermann,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有识别可交换因子算法的理论缺陷，提出修正后的定理和算法以确保正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在概率图模型（如因子图）中利用对象的不可区分性是提升概率推断算法的关键，允许处理域大小相关的可计算概率推断问题。识别可交换因子是这一过程的核心构建块，但现有最先进算法存在理论缺陷。

Method: 1. 分析现有最先进算法识别可交换因子的理论基础；2. 证明现有定理实际上只是必要条件而非充分条件；3. 提出修正版的定理作为识别可交换因子的必要条件；4. 提出修正版的最先进算法，保持效率同时确保正确性；5. 引入具有更紧最坏情况界限的补充算法。

Result: 1. 揭示了现有最先进算法可能产生错误结果；2. 证明了修正版的定理；3. 提出了修正后的算法确保正确性；4. 开发了具有更紧最坏情况界限的补充算法。

Conclusion: 该论文修正了提升概率推断中识别可交换因子的理论基础和算法实现，通过提供正确的定理证明和修正算法解决了现有方法的缺陷，同时引入了性能改进的补充算法。

Abstract: Exploiting the indistinguishability of objects in a probabilistic graphical model such as a factor graph is key to lifted probabilistic inference algorithms and allows for tractable probabilistic inference problems with respect to domain sizes. A central building block for the exploitation of indistinguishable objects in factor graphs is the identification of commutative factors, i.e., factors whose output values are invariant under permutations of input values assigned to a subset of their arguments. In this paper, we revisit the theoretical foundations underlying the state-of-the-art algorithm to detect commutative factors. Specifically, we show that in its current form, the state-of-the-art algorithm relies on a central theorem that is mistakenly regarded as a sufficient condition to identify commutative factors, while it actually only implies necessary condition. Consequently, the state of the art might, as we show in this paper, deliver incorrect results. To fix the flaws currently present in the state of the art, we prove a slightly modified version of the aforementioned theorem, which serves as a necessary condition to identify commutative factors. Moreover, we present a corrected version of the state-of-the-art algorithm, which keeps its efficiency while ensuring correctness and introduce a complementary algorithm with tighter worst-case bounds.

</details>


### [135] [TADDLE: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26911)
*Hanqi Duan,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TADDLE是一个工具增强的智能体系统，用于检测LLM生成的同行评审中的缺陷，并发布了首个专家标注的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的同行评审日益普遍，但现有方法存在局限性：要么只能分类作者身份而不评估质量，要么使用为人类撰写评审设计的特征来评分质量。目前缺乏能在单个缺陷类型层面检测LLM生成评审缺陷的系统。

Method: 提出TADDLE系统，将检测任务分解为四个专用分析工具（验证、纠正、完成和转换），由一个智能体协调。通过两阶段半监督学习的集成器将这些工具输出合成二进制和多标签分类。创建了包含1,800个评审（针对50篇ICLR 2025论文）的专家标注基准，由18位领域专家按照六种缺陷类别（加上非缺陷标签）的多标签标注。

Result: 广泛实验表明，TADDLE在二进制检测和多标签分类任务上都表现出色。系统代码和基准数据集已开源发布。

Conclusion: TADDLE填补了在个体缺陷类型层面检测LLM生成同行评审缺陷的空白，为这一重要任务提供了有效的工具和评估基准。

Abstract: LLM-generated peer reviews are increasingly common at major venues, yet their deficiencies are hard to detect because they are uniformly fluent and well-structured. Existing work either classifies authorship without judging quality, or scores quality with features designed for human-written reviews; no prior system detects deficiencies in LLM-generated reviews at the level of individual defect types. To bridge the gap, we introduce TADDLE, a Tool-Augmented Agent for Detecting Deficient LLM-Generated Peer Reviews, together with the first expert-annotated benchmark for this task. Our benchmark comprises 1,800 reviews on 50 ICLR 2025 papers, multi-label-annotated by 18 domain experts against a taxonomy of six defect categories (plus a non-deficient label). TADDLE decomposes detection into four specialized analysis tools -- Verify, Correct, Complete, and Transform -- orchestrated by an agent; an integrator synthesizes their outputs into binary and multi-label classifications via two-stage semi-supervised learning. Extensive experiments show that TADDLE performs strongly on both binary detection and the multi-label classification task. We release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/AquariusAQ/TADDLE.

</details>


### [136] [Neuro-Symbolic Verification of LLM Outputs for Data-Sensitive Domains (extended preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26942)
*Paul Sigloch,Christoph Benzmüller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合验证架构，结合形式化符号方法与神经语义分析，为LLM生成内容提供互补保证，应用于医疗设备损伤评估系统，显著减少幻觉并提高效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键领域部署面临可靠性挑战：幻觉、不一致性和隐私漏洞带来不可接受的风险，特别是在涉及法律、财务或安全后果的场景中

Method: 采用混合验证架构，结合形式化符号方法（逻辑推理用于输入验证，利用完备性特性提供结构化要求的可判定保证）和基于嵌入的语义相似性（用于输出验证，检测上下文幻觉）。实现为并行的基于参与者的管道，解决基于提示的自我验证方法的局限性

Result: 在HAIMEDA医疗设备损伤评估报告系统中验证，结构化实体的幻觉检测率超过83%，语义伪造检测率72%，报告创建时间减少30%

Conclusion: 神经符号架构可以为LLM在数据敏感领域的部署提供有原则的安全保障，混合验证方法能有效应对LLM的可靠性挑战

Abstract: LLMs deployed in high-stakes domains face fundamental reliability challenges: hallucinations, inconsistencies, and privacy vulnerabilities introduce unacceptable risks where errors carry legal, financial, or safety consequences. This paper presents a hybrid verification architecture combining formal symbolic methods with neural semantic analysis to provide complementary guarantees for LLM-generated content. This architecture employs logical reasoning for input verification, leveraging completeness properties to provide decidable guarantees on structured requirements. For output validation, embedding-based semantic similarity detects contextual hallucinations where formal methods lack expressiveness. This separation is realized in a parallel, actor-based pipeline, addressing limitations of prompt-based self-verification approaches, which inherit the distributional biases that produce hallucinations. The proposed architecture and type-aware verification method are validated with HAIMEDA, a real-world medical device damage assessment reporting system developed through Action Design Research. Evaluation shows hallucination detection rates of over 83% for structured entities and 72% for semantic fabrications, with a 30% reduction in report creation time, demonstrating that neuro-symbolic architectures can provide principled safeguards for LLM deployment in data-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [137] [LELA: An End-to-end LLM-based Entity Linking Framework with Zero-shot Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26956)
*Samy Haffoudhi,Nikola Dobričić,Fabian Suchanek,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LELA（一个模块化、领域无关的LLM实体消歧方法）扩展为实用的Python库，集成零样本命名实体识别，提供端到端的实体链接管道


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体链接方法通常与特定的目标知识库和领域绑定，限制了其实际应用，需要更通用的解决方案

Method: 扩展LELA方法（基于LLM的模块化、领域无关实体消歧方法）为Python库，集成零样本NER，构建完整的端到端实体链接管道

Result: 提供了验证LELA性能和鲁棒性的实验结果，并开发了可供用户交互的演示系统

Conclusion: 成功开发了一个实用的、领域无关的实体链接库，解决了现有方法的局限性，适用于真实世界应用

Abstract: Entity linking is a key component of many downstream NLP systems, yet existing approaches are often tied to the specific target knowledge bases and domains, limiting their real world application. In this paper, we extend LELA, a modular and domain-agnostic LLM-based entity disambiguation method, into a practical Python library that integrates zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (NER) -thereby providing a complete end-toend pipeline for entity-linking in real-world usage. We provide experimental results validating LELA's performance and robustness across diverse entity linking settings. In our demo, users can play with the system on their own input texts.

</details>


### [138] [Generating Robust Portfolios of Optimization Models using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27013)
*Eleni Straitouri,Cheol Woo Kim,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖算法，从自然语言描述生成优化模型组合，利用大语言模型既作为随机生成器又作为推理评估器的双重角色，确保组合包含高质量候选模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的优化模型构建需要领域专业知识和优化知识，这些往往稀缺；现有基于大语言模型的方法只输出单一模型存在风险，因为没有保证其可靠性

Method: 提出统一框架，将大语言模型同时用作随机生成器和推理评估器，生成优化模型组合，利用两种能力的互补性；提供理论保证确保组合包含高质量候选

Result: 经验验证表明该方法在一系列优化建模任务中表现优异，实现了理论承诺的实际效果

Conclusion: 该方法为大语言模型在优化建模中的应用提供了新范式，通过生成多样化候选模型组合降低风险，支持决策者在审查多个选项后进行选择的 principled 人机协作流程

Abstract: Mathematical optimization is a powerful tool for structured decision-making across domains such as resource allocation and planning. Formulating optimization models faithful to reality, though, remains a significant bottleneck as it typically demands both domain expertise and optimization knowledge that are often scarce. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) promise to bridge this gap, enabling the generation of candidate optimization models from natural language descriptions. However, there is no guarantee that any single LLM-generated model is reliable, and existing approaches that output only one model are therefore risky. In this work, we propose a novel algorithm that generates a portfolio of optimization models, designed to be robust to the limitations of LLMs. Our method exploits the observation that a single LLM can play two distinct roles $\unicode{x2014}$ as a stochastic generator and as a reasoning evaluator $\unicode{x2014}$ and proposes a unified framework that leverages both capabilities in a complementary manner. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that, as long as either the generator or the evaluator is well-aligned with human preferences, the portfolio is guaranteed to contain high-quality candidates, enabling a principled human-in-the-loop process in which a decision-maker can review multiple candidates before committing to one. We further validate our approach empirically, demonstrating strong performance across a range of optimization modeling tasks.

</details>


### [139] [Boosting Knowledge Graph Foundation Models via Enhanced Negative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27023)
*Yinan Liu,Wenjin Xu,Zhiyuan Zha,Xiaochun Yang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KMAS方法，一种自适应负采样方法，用于增强知识图谱基础模型。通过使用关系编码器生成的关系嵌入来构建困难负三元组，并在训练过程中动态调整困难负样本比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱基础模型在零样本知识图谱补全中受到广泛关注，但现有方法使用随机负三元组进行训练，质量有限，为模型训练提供弱监督信号，限制了模型性能提升。

Method: 提出KMAS自适应负采样方法：1）通过现有KGFM关系编码器生成的关系嵌入构建困难负三元组；2）在训练过程中动态调整困难负样本比例：预热阶段后先线性增加比例，再线性减少，以适配模型能力演化。

Result: 在44个数据集上的大量实验表明，提出的负采样方法能够显著增强多种先进的知识图谱基础模型，而不需要过多的额外时间或内存消耗。

Conclusion: KMAS是一种简单而有效的自适应负采样方法，能够通过高质量的困难负样本增强现有知识图谱基础模型的性能，解决了随机负采样监督信号弱的问题。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) have become the core backbone of numerous downstream tasks such as question answering and recommender systems. However, despite all this, KGs are often very incomplete. To perform zero-shot knowledge graph completion in unseen KGs, which have different relational vocabularies from those used for pre-training, KG foundation models (KGFMs) receive a wide range of attention. Existing KGFMs often perform training using random negative triples, which are constructed by replacing the head or tail entity of a positive triple with a random entity. However, these negative triples are often constructed with limited quality, providing weak supervision for KGFM training. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective adaptive negative sampling approach, KMAS, to enhance existing KGFMs. KMAS constructs hard negative triples through the updated relation embeddings generated from the existing KGFM's relation encoder. To further adaptively align with the evolving capability of the KGFM during the training process, KMAS adjusts the ratio of hard negative triples dynamically throughout the whole training process: after a warmup phrase, it increases the ratio linearly and then decreases linearly. Extensive experiments are conducted over 44 data sets. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed negative sampling method can enhance many SOTA KGFMs without requiring excessive additional time or memory consumption.

</details>


### [140] [BatteryMFormer: Multi-level Learning for Battery Degradation Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27044)
*Ruifeng Tan,Jintao Dong,Weixiang Hong,Jia Li,Jiaqiang Huang,Tong-Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BatteryMFormer——一种用于早期电池退化轨迹预测的多级Transformer模型，通过整合老化条件感知解码器、元退化模式记忆库和双视角编码器，显著提升了电池寿命预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期电池退化轨迹预测对于电池优化、制造和部署至关重要。现有方法未能充分建模电池退化数据的两个关键特征：1）数据具有多层次结构（包括老化条件内的规律和电池间的轨迹模式）；2）电压-电流曲线的退化相关变化通常局限在特定荷电状态区间内。

Method: 提出了BatteryMFormer模型，包含三个核心组件：1）老化条件感知解码器，通过老化条件信息查询和老化条件感知注意力注入老化先验知识；2）元退化模式记忆库，学习并检索轨迹原型来指导长期预测；3）双视角编码器，从电压和电流时间序列中联合捕获时间动态和SOC局部化变化。

Result: 在四个电池领域的广泛实验表明，BatteryMFormer一致性地优于最先进的基准方法，标志着向可靠电池退化轨迹预测迈出了重要一步。

Conclusion: BatteryMFormer通过显式建模电池退化数据的关键特征（多层次结构和SOC局部化变化），为早期电池退化轨迹预测提供了一个有效的框架，在多个领域验证了其优越性能。代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Early battery degradation trajectory forecasting (BDTF), which predicts the full-life state-of-health trajectory from early operational data, is critical for battery optimization, manufacturing, and deployment. Battery degradation data exhibit two key characteristics. First, degradation data present a multi-level structure, including regularities shared within aging conditions and trajectory patterns shared across batteries. Second, degradation-related variations in voltage-current profiles are often localized to specific state-of-charge (SOC) intervals. Existing approaches often fail to explicitly model these characteristics. To bridge this gap, we propose BatteryMFormer, a multi-level Transformer for early BDTF. BatteryMFormer integrates (1) an aging-condition-aware decoder that injects aging-condition priors via aging-condition-informed queries and aging-condition-aware attention, (2) a meta degradation pattern memory that learns and retrieves trajectory prototypes to guide long-horizon forecasting, and (3) a dual-view encoder that jointly captures temporal dynamics and SOC-localized variations from voltage and current time series. Extensive experiments on four battery domains show that BatteryMFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, marking a significant step toward reliable BDTF. Our code is available at https://github.com/Ruifeng-Tan/BatteryMFormer.

</details>


### [141] [Can Broad Biomedical Knowledge be Contextualized into Scenario-Grounded Propositions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27082)
*Qingyuan Zeng,Ziyang Chen,Pengxiang Cai,Zixin Guan,Anglin Liu,Lang Qin,Xinyao Lai,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCENE是一个双层多智能体框架，将生物医学知识情境化视为迭代搜索过程，旨在连接广泛的生物医学知识与具体的实验或临床数据，生成可验证的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学发现需要连接广泛的生物医学知识与具体的实验或临床数据。背景知识虽然能提示相关机制，但通常过于通用，难以直接映射到数据集变量；而数据驱动模式可能过于数据集特异性且难以进行机制解释。研究人员将这种缺失的联系视为"知识情境化"问题。

Method: 提出SCENE双层多智能体框架：上层将广泛知识转化为搜索方向并基于数据集模式进行落地；下层通过多目标优化执行这些方向，识别平衡证据强度和数据支持的具象命题。两个层级间通过反馈机制迭代优化搜索过程。

Result: 在两个场景中评估：1）临床试验中患者亚组异质性治疗效益发现：SCENE发现具体、良好支持的亚组，优于现有基线方法；2）LINCS L1000研究中情境特异性生物响应识别：SCENE识别出具有强靶点-响应匹配和高阳性率的扰动情境。

Conclusion: SCENE成功连接了广泛知识与情境特异性证据，产生可追溯、可检查的假设供后续验证，为解决生物医学知识情境化问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Biomedical discovery often requires connecting broad biomedical knowledge with specific experimental or clinical data. Background knowledge suggests relevant mechanisms but is usually too general to map directly onto dataset variables, while data-driven patterns can be dataset-specific and hard to interpret mechanistically. We study this missing link as knowledge contextualization: transforming broad biomedical knowledge into evidence-supported, scenario-grounded propositions that domain experts can inspect, replay, and validate. We propose SCENE, a bi-level multi-agent framework that treats knowledge contextualization as iterative search. The upper level converts broad knowledge into search directions and grounds them in the dataset schema. The lower level executes these directions through multi-objective optimization to identify concrete propositions that balance evidential strength and data support. Feedback between the two levels progressively refines the search. We evaluate SCENE in two settings: discovering patient subgroups with heterogeneous treatment benefits in clinical trial scenarios, and identifying context-specific biological responses in LINCS L1000 studies. In clinical trials, SCENE discovers specific, well-supported subgroups and outperforms existing baselines. In L1000 studies, SCENE identifies perturbational contexts with strong target-response matching and high positive rates. These results show that SCENE bridges broad knowledge and scenario-specific evidence, producing traceable, inspectable hypotheses for follow-up validation.

</details>


### [142] [Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for General Capability Recovery with Domain Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27115)
*Tianlei Chen,Jiao Ou,Ziyuan Liu,Ruiming Tang,Jian Liang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaMOPD提出一种新方法解决领域专业化导致大语言模型通用能力下降的问题，通过解耦交替训练和基于差距的样本选择来有效恢复通用能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专业化通常会削弱LLM的通用能力，现有的多教师策略蒸馏方法假设教师模型训练数据分布已知，但这个假设在实际应用中难以满足，特别是当通用教师是开源模型且后训练数据未知时

Method: 提出Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (CaMOPD)，包含两个关键技术：1) 解耦交替训练，分别为通用能力恢复和领域能力保留提供专用更新；2) 基于差距的样本选择，优先选择师生对数概率差距较大的样本以集中修正信号

Result: 在角色扮演对话和医学推理QA两个场景中，CaMOPD在通用能力恢复方面优于基准方法，同时保持领域特定行为。梯度一致性分析进一步支持CaMOPD在产生更一致的修正信号方面的预期效果

Conclusion: CaMOPD通过处理不完全提示覆盖情况下的两个关键故障模式（恢复-保留抵消效应和弱信号扁平化），提供了一种有效的通用能力恢复方法，无需依赖教师模型训练数据分布的完整知识

Abstract: Domain specialization can improve LLM behavior in vertical domains, but often weakens the general capabilities inherited from the original model. Recent Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) pipelines recover model capabilities by supervising student-generated trajectories with teacher feedback, but typically assume teacher-aligned prompt coverage, requiring prompts to match the teachers' training distributions. This assumption is difficult to satisfy when the general teacher is an open-source model whose post-training data are unknown. Instead of attempting to reconstruct this hidden distribution, we study general capability recovery with readily available proxy general prompts. We identify two failure modes of vanilla MOPD in this incomplete-coverage situation: recovery-preservation counteraction from mixing conflicting recovery and preservation gradients, and weak-signal flattening from uniformly averaging samples with unequal correction demand. We propose Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (CaMOPD), which addresses these issues with decoupled alternating training and gap-based sample selection. CaMOPD gives general recovery dedicated updates, periodically reviews domain prompts for preservation, and selects samples with larger averaged token-level teacher-student log-probability gaps to concentrate correction signals. Across role-play dialogue and medical reasoning QA scenarios, CaMOPD performs best in general recovery over baselines while maintaining domain-specific behavior. Gradient coherence analyses further support the intended effect of CaMOPD in producing more coherent correction signals.

</details>


### [143] [ICCU: In-Context Continual Unlearning via Pattern-Induced Refusal Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27138)
*Ruihao Pan,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICCU提出了一种基于上下文持续学习的机器遗忘框架，通过从遗忘数据集中提取可读的拒绝规则并在推理时应用，避免了修改模型参数，实现了顺序遗忘请求的高效处理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，语言模型的遗忘请求通常按顺序到达，现有基于微调的方法面临三大挑战：1）每个请求的微调成本高；2）累积的效用损失；3）可能引起跨请求干扰。需要一种高效、可扩展的持续遗忘解决方案

Method: ICCU框架首先从遗忘数据集中归纳出可读的拒绝规则，然后在推理时将这些规则作为过滤器或通过系统提示应用，无需修改模型参数。规则以顺序无关的并集形式累积，确保组合性和避免跨请求干扰

Result: 实验表明ICCU能有效抑制目标知识同时保持模型效用，能够跨顺序请求扩展，并且对转述和跨语言查询保持鲁棒性。在规则归纳完成后，原始遗忘集数据可以被丢弃

Conclusion: ICCU为顺序遗忘请求提供了一种高效、可扩展且无干扰的解决方案，通过基于上下文的规则应用机制，克服了传统微调方法在持续学习场景中的局限性

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific data from trained language models. In real-world deployments, unlearning requests often arrive sequentially, which challenges existing fine-tuning-based methods: fine-tuning each request is costly, accumulates utility loss, and may cause cross-request interference. To address these issues, we propose ICCU (In-Context Continual Unlearning), an in-context continual unlearning framework that induces readable refusal rules from unlearning datasets and applies them at inference time either as a filter or via the system prompt, without modifying model parameters. Because rules are accumulated as an order-independent union, ICCU is compositional and free of cross-request interference, and the original forget-set data can be discarded after rule induction. Extensive experiments show that ICCU effectively suppresses target knowledge while preserving utility, scales across sequential requests, and remains robust to paraphrased and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [144] [VitaBench 2.0: Evaluating Personalized and Proactive Agents in Long-Term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27141)
*Yuxin Chen,Yi Zhang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Zhiyuan Yao,Chenhang Cui,Jingnan Zheng,Yaqi Huo,Xi Su,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VitaBench 2.0是一个用于评估个性化主动智能体的新基准，重点关注从碎片化用户交互中推断和利用用户偏好的能力，填补现有基准忽略真实场景中用户偏好建模的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型演变为与现实用户协作的智能体，有效协作越来越依赖于理解用户未明确表达的意图。用户意图通常反映在碎片化的日常交互中，需要个性化建模和主动交互。然而，现有智能体基准主要评估推理和工具使用能力，很大程度上忽视了在真实场景中推断和利用用户偏好的挑战。

Method: 作者提出了VitaBench 2.0基准，其中任务组织为针对个体用户的时间有序序列，用户偏好嵌入在碎片化和异质的交互中。任务完成要求智能体从这些交互中持续提取、利用和更新用户偏好。此外，通过要求智能体识别缺失信息并在决策前主动从用户或环境获取信息的任务来评估主动性。为支持系统分析，提供了可扩展的内存接口，支持不同内存架构的受控比较。

Result: 对多个前沿专有和开源LLM进行基准测试，结果表明现实世界的个性化对最先进的模型仍然极具挑战性，揭示了当前能力与实际需求之间的显著差距。广泛分析进一步揭示了当前智能体在真实世界个性化决策中的失败模式和能力瓶颈。

Conclusion: 现实世界的个性化对当前智能体仍然是重大挑战，VitaBench 2.0基准揭示了现有模型的能力瓶颈，为未来模型改进提供了洞察。该基准填补了智能体评估中个性化建模和主动交互能力评估的重要空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have evolved into interactive agents that collaborate with users in real-world tasks. Effective collaboration in such settings increasingly depends on understanding the user beyond what is explicitly stated, as user intent is often reflected in fragmented daily interactions and requires both personalized modeling and proactive interaction. However, existing agent benchmarks primarily evaluate reasoning and tool use, largely overlooking the challenges of inferring and leveraging user preferences in realistic scenarios. To address this gap, we introduce VitaBench 2.0, a benchmark for evaluating personalized and proactive agent behavior in long-term user interactions. In VitaBench 2.0, tasks are organized as temporally ordered sequences for individual users, where preferences are embedded in fragmented and heterogeneous interactions. Successful completion of tasks requires the agent to continuously extract, utilize, and update user preferences from these interactions. We further evaluate proactiveness through tasks that require agents to recognize missing information and actively acquire it from users or environments before making decisions. To support systematic analysis, we provide an extensible memory interface that enables controlled comparison across different memory architectures. We benchmark a diverse set of frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs. Results show that real-world personalization remains highly challenging even for state-of-the-art models, revealing a substantial gap between current capabilities and practical requirements. Extensive analysis further reveals the failure modes and capability bottlenecks of current agents in real-world personalized decision-making, providing insights for future model improvements.

</details>


### [145] [Detecting Is Not Resolving: The Monitoring Control Gap in Retrieval Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27157)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Chen Ye,Xuyang Teng,Bo Yang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现检索增强型LLMs存在"监测-控制"缺口：尽管模型能够识别矛盾证据，但这种认知无法约束其最终决策行为，导致单轮诊断高估RAG安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估协议假设单轮鲁棒性能够预测多轮证据累积时的鲁棒性，但这一假设在检索增强型LLMs部署于证据质量决定行动安全性的任务中存在根本缺陷

Method: 1. 设计多轮文档累积评估协议，覆盖四个模型家族（1.5B-32B参数），进行超过50,000次轮级评估；2. 采用隐藏状态探测、注意力分析和响应策略分类等多种机制分析方法；3. 结合针对性人工验证

Result: 1. 单轮诊断系统性高估RAG安全性；2. 矛盾识别与安全解决之间不存在相关性；3. 不存在通用的提示修复方法；4. 机制证据表明危险相关信息在内部得到表征并获得增强关注，但无法约束输出行为；5. 动作选择是缺陷最可能的根源

Conclusion: 模型认知与行为之间存在根本性差距，在检索增强系统应用于高风险场景前，必须测量并弥合这一"监测-控制"缺口，仅靠识别矛盾证据不足以确保安全决策

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented LLMs are deployed for tasks where evidence quality determines action safety, yet evaluation protocols assume that single-turn robustness predicts robustness when evidence accumulates across turns. We show this assumption is fundamentally incorrect. Models exhibit a monitoring-control gap: they readily acknowledge contradictory evidence, yet this awareness fails to constrain their final recommendations - detecting epistemic conflict does not imply resolving it safely. Through a multi-turn document accumulation protocol across four model families (1.5B-32B parameters) and over 50,000 turn-level evaluations, we demonstrate that single-turn diagnostics systematically overestimate RAG safety, that contradiction acknowledgement is uncorrelated with safe resolution, a pattern corroborated by targeted human validation, and that no universal prompt fix exists. Converging mechanism evidence - hidden-state probing, attention analysis, and response-strategy taxonomy - points to action selection as the most plausible locus of the deficit: danger-relevant information is internally represented and receives enhanced attention during unsafe generation, yet fails to constrain output behavior. The gap between what models recognize and what they do must be measured and closed before retrieval-augmented systems can be trusted in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [146] [Query Symbolically or Retrieve Semantically? A Dataset and Method for Semi-Structured Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27164)
*Mateusz Czyżnikiewicz,Ryszard Tuora,Adam Kozakiewicz,Tomasz Ziętkiewicz,Mateusz Galiński,Michał Godziszewski,Michał Karpowicz,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DualGraph是一个RAG框架，通过文本知识图和符号知识图的双重表示来处理半结构化文档的问答任务，在SpecsQA基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统主要基于查询和文档块之间的语义相似性进行检索，这在处理非结构化文本时有效，但对于半结构化语料库（如产品文档）不太可靠，因为回答需要精确筛选、聚合或在结构化属性上进行详尽检索。符号方法支持这类操作，但在嘈杂的自然语言语料库上往往脆弱。

Method: DualGraph框架通过两种互补视图表示文档：1）用于语义检索的文本知识图；2）用于在类型化主-谓-宾三元组上进行符号查询的符号知识图。基于这两个组件，提供了多种策略来选择或组合语义和符号证据。

Result: 在SpecsQA基准测试（来自商业购物网站的半结构化产品文档和人工策划的问题）上的实验表明，DualGraph在各种问题类型上一致优于最先进的密集检索、GraphRAG、符号和面向表格的基线方法。

Conclusion: DualGraph通过结合语义和符号检索的优势，有效解决了半结构化文档问答中的检索挑战，提供了一个鲁棒的框架来处理需要精确筛选、聚合和跨文档结构化查询的复杂问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for question answering typically retrieve evidence by semantic similarity between the query and document chunks. While effective for unstructured text, this approach is less reliable on semi-structured corpora where answering may require exact filtering, aggregation, or exhaustive retrieval over structured attributes across multiple documents. Symbolic approaches support such operations, but they are often brittle on noisy natural-language corpora. We address this gap with DualGraph, a RAG framework that represents documents through two complementary views: a Textual Knowledge Graph for semantic retrieval and a Symbolic Knowledge Graph for symbolic querying over typed subject--predicate--object triples. Building on these two components, we provide multiple strategies for selecting or combining semantic and symbolic evidence.We also introduce SpecsQA, a benchmark from a commercial shopping website with semi-structured product documents and manually curated questions spanning open-ended and specification-oriented retrieval.
